设计/计划的失败展示由 `AiChatPanel/planningFailureScope.ts` 限定为当前业务阶段、当前本地会话承接的精确 source Run/Graph thread；无会话、空会话、其他执行和其他阶段不借用应用级 Planning 错误、连接恢复卡或重试。会话选择仍按阶段最近一次记录，不搜索其他会话；后端确认入口继续独立读取原始 Planning 状态，不受失败展示归属限制。回归：`pnpm test:connection-recovery` 的 `planningFailureScope.test.ts`。 规划失败归属读取当前会话最新消息中的 Workflow，不能优先采用普通聊天 Hook 留下的旧 Run 缓存；独立 Planning Runtime 的本轮中断消息已落盘时应立即显示对应重试。

技术规划右侧阅读面板在 Workflow 缺少有效架构快照时，打开即读取当前正式 TechnicalPlan JSON 与 ProductPlan，不依赖冷恢复标记；`planningArtifactRecovery.ts::technicalPlanFromWorkflow` 排除节点重置产生的空对象，避免遮住结果快照或磁盘文件。该读取不推进工作流或提交确认。回归：`tests/technicalPlanArtifact.test.ts` 与 Electron 计划阶段阅读面板。

设计正式修订通过 `planning_stage_entry` 后进入技术规划时，`application_revision_lifecycle.py` 接受设计修订的待入口生命周期；`application_technical_planning.py` 沿已有设计 revision 生成，不重新申请技术专项 revision。Native Recovery 的 `execution_recovery_executor.py` 同步验证设计分支 change/target 和入口状态，仍保持精确 Run/Thread、checkpoint 和确认门。`technical_planning_retry.py` 清空 interaction 时保留同一生成请求，避免恢复事务边界读回旧请求。回归：`tests.test_technical_planning_conversation`、`tests.test_technical_planning_stage_retry`。

聊天展示不再使用 `PhasePendingCard` 或按空消息推断 loading 的 helper。`MessageList` 的 loading 仅由当前阶段真实执行驱动，其他会话操作锁不能作为加载状态；残留占位和本机无历史均不生成恢复/失败卡。当前确认区独立读取后端 Native Interrupt 并展示 `WorkflowRunCard`，不依赖本地会话、消息数量或快照回填；历史确认只读，当前同门禁历史卡不重复显示。进入工作台不再为展示确认回填聊天消息，失败与中断仍由底部原错误/Backend ActionPlan 提供重试。正式修订确认后的 continuation 仍由后端 change/branch/token 授权；本机无原设计或开发会话时创建开发输出会话，不把历史缺失当作业务拒绝。回归：`pnpm test:workflow-preview` 的空历史、旧消息未回填、门禁去重和空问题列表确认用例，以及 `pnpm test:application-planning-runtime`。

阶段历史展示统一由 `hooks/phaseSessionSelection.ts` 与 `hooks/useChatSessions.ts` 按当前阶段选择 `updatedAt` 最新的本地会话，不以修订、Graph Thread、消息数量或上次浏览选择筛选。显式业务交接仅在该次创建/进入时指定目标；冷进入不补建会话或来源回执，不依赖本机原开发会话。无会话是有效空态，不能阻塞工作台或推断正式资产缺失；已有文件资产继续独立加载。`AiChatPanel` 仅在当前业务阶段绑定实时规划事件，历史消息保持自己的 Workflow 快照，不以同 Graph Thread 的新修订覆盖旧计划卡；确认/执行仍走现有后端门禁。回归：`pnpm test:design-conversation`、`pnpm test:application-planning-runtime`，并在 Electron 验证二次修订期间切回旧计划及无本地会话空态。

联合需求文档中的受限操作归属问题沿 `requirement_document_review` 接受 `answer`，回到 ProductPlan 确定性补齐映射后仍需联合确认；配置投影不一致沿同一标准确认卡展示诊断，复用编辑/修订/确认，确认时继续检查唯一 application.json 投影。不得由特殊问题状态绕过联合确认或形成不支持提交的门。回归：`tests.test_requirement_document_special_interactions`。

需求正式修订的生成游标在 RequirementSpec 草稿就绪时即推进到 ProductPlan，沿联合确认例外先完成两份草稿；`nodes/product_planning.py` 对 checkpoint 中待执行的产品修订跳过旧稿展示/修补捷径，以旧稿和新上游生成完整新版。只有联合确认后才推进 UI。回归：`tests.test_application_design_conversation`、`tests.test_product_planning_retry`。

需求/设计正式二次修改的批准先经 `services/design_revision_admission.py` 冻结为后端私有 `design_revision_intent`，协议入口不消费 pending impact；Graph 将输入写入 checkpoint 后，`application_planning_revision.py::analyze_design_intent` 才幂等批准并回退到最早产物。批准后但尚未进入需求节点的中断沿原 design-intent Node Entry 走显式 Native Recovery，PRE_OWNERSHIP 仅按原 change/impact/thread 与 lifecycle revision 领取运行归属；不比较后续修改要求。凭据在意图节点完成后清空，公开 Workflow State 排除该字段。回归：`tests.test_design_revision_recovery`。

规划只读查询没有任何执行存档时，`protocols/application_page_planning.py` 返回正常 AG-UI 完成信封与 `checkpointAvailable=false`，不生成失败或恢复动作。Renderer `applicationPlanningRecovery.ts` 将其视为无快照；Runtime/reducer 清除旧 Workflow、Recovery 与错误并保持连接健康，`AiChatPanel` 不再回显历史恢复错误。已有但为空的存档与连接异常仍作为真实错误处理。该分支不修改 lifecycle、删除项目资产或自动重启已有应用；首次创建保留原生成入口。回归：`tests.test_application_planning_recovery_projection`、`pnpm test:application-planning-runtime`。

规划冷启动在 `applicationPlanningRuntime.ts::ensureStarted` 成功读取现有 checkpoint 后只展示权威状态，不因 lifecycle 仍为 running 或 ActionPlan 尚未签发而落入普通阶段生成；仍运行的执行保持进度，已中断的执行沿原底部 Backend ActionPlan 显式重试。仅 collecting_requirement/pending 且 checkpoint 缺失时允许首次启动。回归：`pnpm test:application-planning-runtime`（冷启动 running 无动作→只读对账出恢复动作、全程不自动 send）。

冷恢复首次读取早于旧租约到期时，`useApplicationPlanningRuntimes.ts` 每5秒调用 Runtime 的只读 `refreshDetachedRunningState`：仅本地无执行流、transport idle 且 Backend 分类仍为 running 时读取权威快照，直到对账为中断/确认/终态；已存在本地写流、正在同步、状态不确定和已释放实例不参与。该读取不会提交 Graph 或恢复动作，避免租约已过期但当前页面永久停在旧 running。回归：`pnpm test:application-planning-runtime` T3/T4。

正式修订启动的展示会话撤销先经现有 AG-UI lifecycle 只读确认：已接纳同一 branch/impact/source 或后端暂不可达时保留目标 StageSession 和来源回执，不能因 SSE 断线删除它们。`revisionSession.ts` 继续承载显式业务交接的来源校验；普通阶段展示不再调用 `useFormalRevisionHandoffRecovery.ts` 补建或强制定位会话。回归：`formalRevisionHandoffRecovery.test.ts`（由 `pnpm test:application-planning-runtime` 执行）。

正式修订确认沿用原 Conversation 卡片的 `summary.target` / `state.change_target`，不按点击时的页面选择重算目标。`application_page_planning.py` 的入场拒绝同样发布包含 failed summary 的 Workflow AG-UI 投影；`applicationPlanningRuntime.ts` 将该终态抛回交接调用方，复用原回滚逻辑撤销未入场会话/回执并在来源会话显示原因，保留原确认出口。此修复不修改 Agent 节点、不添加 Lifecycle 校验。回归：`tests.test_design_revision_recovery`、`pnpm test:application-planning-runtime`、`pnpm test:connection-recovery`。

技术专项修订也由同一个 `ApplicationPlanningRuntime` 发送 `start_technical_revision`，与设计修订共用阶段会话准备及入场失败回滚；聊天 Hook 不再另发该请求。所有新规划事件先更新应用 Canonical Workflow，再投递阶段消息，确认区、进度和右侧文档读取同一份新状态，避免旧的已确认规划遮住新 interrupt。回归覆盖已确认旧规划→新技术修订待确认，以及两类修订的卡片目标归属。

技术规划已开始时，`workbenchPhase.ts` 的展示阶段优先采用当前技术阶段，不能被修订里滞后的 `currentArtifact=ui-design` 拉回设计阶段。`AiChatPanel` 在绑定当前阶段会话后补接 Canonical Workflow，空会话也接入失败快照，使重连后的精确 Run 重试入口可见；浏览其他阶段仍不接入当前失败。`planningArtifactRecovery.ts::technicalPlanBaselineStatus` 将生成中/失败时的已确认基线标为上一版，避免右侧误报本轮已确认。只改前端展示与恢复接线，不改变 Agent 或 Lifecycle 准入。回归：`pnpm test:workflow-preview`、`pnpm test:connection-recovery`、`pnpm test:design-runtime`。

工作台冷进入时，缓存的 ready_for_workbench 可能早于正式修订生成的新待确认产物；`pages/WorkbenchPage.tsx` 在拟报产物损坏前通过现有 lifecycle AG-UI 读取当前状态，规划中/待确认时保留入口，防止用旧缓存将正常修订拒之门外。

`protocols/application_page_planning.py` 的 INTERRUPTED 恢复投影与兜底执行器统一使用 `InterruptedTargetResolver` 读取最新已提交 checkpoint，不以 `aget_state` 叠加 pending writes 后的空 next 判定生成已完成；只有已提交的确认门或终态才做终态对账。回归：`tests.test_technical_planning_stage_retry` 的暂停后空 next 展示快照仍签发原生成节点恢复动作。

同一次正式技术修订中的再次修改与 Native Recovery，不再把当前技术规划请求、当前产物审阅 gate 与最初影响确认时的请求和 gate 比较。`services/application_revision_lifecycle.py` 的 active revision begin 与 `services/execution_recovery_executor.py` 的 INPUT_COMMITTED 入场仅据原 change/branch/target 证明修订归属；通用 Run/Thread、精确 checkpoint/节点、当前事务边界及 lifecycle/资源归属校验继续生效。首次 pending impact 的确认合同不变，新产物仍需重新确认。回归：`tests.test_technical_planning_stage_retry` 的正常生成与 begin 失败后原节点恢复用例。

任务计划待确认时显式 End，`services/planning_refresh_recovery.py` 按 Pending 的精确 `workflow_run_id` 对照 `endedExecutions`，暂时将草稿投影为不可行动：保留草稿但释放其全应用确认占用，避免结束后“继续任务”被 Pending 锁置灰。原任务继续入场消费归档后，重新经过原任务计划确认门；不自动确认草稿。回归：`tests.test_planning_refresh_recovery`。

技术规划自由输入沿原 `/application-page-planning/run` 的 `forwardedProps.technicalPlanningConversation`：`ChatComposer/productConversation.ts` 将纯正文 `request` 与执行目标 `conversationTarget` 分开提交，@ 候选在设计阶段为需求设计/UI设计，在计划阶段增加技术规划；新标签替换旧标签，空正文禁发，候选 Enter 不同时发送。指定目标时后端确定性选择分支并携带正文执行，跳过意图分类模型；需求/UI 仍经原设计 Policy 保留上游确认条件，跨阶段先交接独立 DESIGN 线程。指定技术规划时沿原 begin/generate/commit/review，即使暂无基线也可在上游条件满足后生成；显式 UI 回退遇到已跳过或尚无设计稿时，`prepare_ui_revision_state` 保留正文与生成动作，`nodes/ui_confirmation.py` 准备骨架后沿既有单页生成器执行；`ui_design_generator.py::generate_page_react_code` 接收本轮视觉指令，ProductPlan 仍是唯一业务事实来源，生成后保持整体 UI 确认门。无目标时保留原有问答、技术修改与上游变更识别及权限语义路由。所有执行保留生命周期、Run/checkpoint 版本、归属和正式产物确认门，文本不替代确认。`/health` 能力元数据用 `targetStartsExecution` 描述显式目标语义，设计入口只接受需求/UI，技术入口接受三种目标且校验目标与动作匹配。卡片修改仍走既有显式动作，End/切换后的迟到请求保护不变。回归：`tests.test_technical_planning_conversation`、`tests.test_product_conversation_policy`、`tests.test_application_design_conversation`、`tests.test_application_planning_interrupts`、`tests.test_stopped_technical_planning_revision`、`pnpm test:application-planning-runtime`、`pnpm test:design-mention`。详见 `docs/DESIGN_CONVERSATION_AGENT.md`。真实模型与 Electron 回退验收、无旧 UI 稿首次执行修复和问答事实覆盖限制见 [显式节点回退验证](EXPLICIT_NODE_ELECTRON_VALIDATION_2026_11_05.md)。

结束任务沿 `/workflow/run` 的原 AG-UI Plan Control：`services/run_workers.py` 用运行 ContextVar 和 `WorkerStateGraph` 登记主图/验收同步节点的完整退出（含 Agent 后的写盘/回滚），`agents/execution_control.py` 为所有工作区 Agent 工厂注入统一模型/工具取消中间件；异步工具调用独立持有登记，外层取消不能提前释放实际同步工作。受控命令默认继承运行身份。`WorkflowRunRegistry.cancel_and_wait` 同时等待外层 Task、同步工作和命令退出，30 秒未完成明确失败，End 不释放锁或返回成功；前端等待预算为 40 秒，`PlanExecutionDock` 全阶段提供 End 和 loading。

显式 End 在 `application-lifecycle.json.endedExecutions` 保存原执行、完整资源声明、正式修订及工作区摘要，释放 active execution/资源锁；普通 discard 不产生继续资格。`services/ended_workspace.py` 哈希源码、配置和正式产物，排除依赖、构建、日志、checkpoint/recovery/runtime 及 lifecycle 本身。`application_lifecycle_payload` 只公开原执行、`workspaceUnchanged` 和顶层 `endedExecutionsCheckedAt`，不公开摘要/工作区内部登记。继续复用 `resumeExecutionRunId`，请求适配器从归档决定节点，Runtime 从原 thread 的精确 Run checkpoint 恢复私有状态，不消费上次 interaction；生命周期 admission 在同一锁内重新验证指纹、原会话/目标与资源，再消费归档。节点复用既有重入整理和确认门，失败 SmallTask 继续仍从集成测试重开。`service/endedTaskAvailability.ts` 合并同 revision 的最新资格；`AiChatPanel` 只为脱离本地流且仍运行中的任务启用轮询，任务结束后停止。点击“继续任务”时，`useWorkflowConversation.ts::handleContinueEndedPlan` 先沿既有 Lifecycle AG-UI 重新读取工作区资格，凭据失效、工作区变化或读取失败均不提交恢复。未变化只展示“继续任务”，变化后展示“重新发起任务”，点击展开原 `NextDevelopmentGuide`；全部初次完成时保留卡片，展示“暂无可开发任务”。回归：`tests.test_end_task_workers`、`tests.test_workflow_run_control`、`pnpm test:development-artifacts`、`pnpm test:connection-recovery`。

集成测试安排 `small_task_repair` 后公开状态保持 `in_progress`，真实检查项失败仍留在矩阵与报告中，只有终止性失败进入 failed lifecycle。`services/small_task_context.py` 从编译诊断按命令 cwd 解析已授权且真实存在的源码候选，优先交给 SmallTask，并传递 `repair_task_plan.strategy`；不扩大 allowedPaths。`graph/nodes/small_task.py::unit_test_repair` 在首次执行、结束任务后继续和 Native Recovery 时统一将权威 `unit_test_repair_task_plan`、`unit_test_report`、`unit_test_results` 映射为 SmallTask 的策略、报告与修订证据，避免通用集成测试上下文覆盖单测计划。`agents/small_task/agent.py` 沿用前端 Agent 的 `DirectModificationMiddleware(required_tools=[execute_tool])`，确保每轮模型请求包含已注册的命令工具；Prompt 对已诊断的依赖缺失先执行既定安装与真实复测，无需源码改动时返回带验证证据的 `already_satisfied`，再由原单测节点复核。SmallTask 使用现有 LangChain ModelCallLimitMiddleware 限制每次任务 24 轮模型调用，超限明确收口为失败；`middleware/small_task_cancellation.py` 和动态文件范围按内部 active Run 观察取消，防止同步修复线程在 End 后继续调用模型或工具。前端 End 不受普通写入只读门禁拦截，先等待本地流落盘/收口，再走原 AG-UI Plan Control；后端按 workspace/精确 Run 校验后等待真实 owner 取消，超时不释放锁。Pending Abandon、正式确认与其他 Run 保持各自边界。回归：`tests.test_unit_test_repair_context`（真实 Graph/结束归档/AG-UI 续接、生产 Agent 工具流安装与复测）、`tests.test_integration_repair_progress`、`tests.test_workflow_run_control`、`pnpm test:connection-recovery`。

TechnicalPlan 的 `repair` 重入基线由 `domain/application_planning_recovery.py::validate_application_planning_baseline` 统一校验，技术事务入口与兜底执行器共用：只有当前 TechnicalPlan 缺失或为空、且 boundary 未声明 baseline 摘要时允许无基线续修；已有计划或已有摘要必须匹配，`revise` 始终要求有效基线。技术规划重试复用原 begin/generate/commit 节点：`services/technical_planning_retry.py` 为显式阶段重试及 Native 失败重试保留服务端 checkpoint 的上次业务要求，优先以当前落盘技术规划为基线，无已有计划则走原生成器的首次生成分支；清空旧候选/错误，重新建立当前输入边界，不把“继续执行”提示作为业务要求。公共重试保留完整事务上下文，从原 begin/generate/commit 失败节点执行；生成输入读取当前磁盘，commit 保留未落盘候选，成功仍暂停在正式确认门。失败审阅的重新生成动作把原生成 Run 写入内部 `technical_plan_repair_source_run_id`；begin 在输入提交后由 `application_revision_lifecycle.py` 校验原归属并 CAS 交接到新 Run，未交接时异常重试也由既有 pre-ownership 规则领取。字段在 begin 消费、generate 返回或新修订重置时清空，并从两处 Workflow 公开投影排除；不作为新的配置或产物来源。回归：`tests.test_technical_planning_stage_retry`、`tests.test_technical_planning_repair_baseline`（真实 Graph、Durable Store 与 AG-UI 恢复链路）。

技术规划 begin/generate/commit 的“停止生成”是可恢复暂停：`protocols/workflow/runtime.py` 保留取消控制回执，同时把 Durable execution 记为 `interrupted`，由现有 InterruptedTargetResolver 和恢复动作继续；工作台取消与结束保持原语义。`service/applicationPlanningRecovery.ts` 接受后端当前合法的 `cancelled/stopped` 终态投影，但不为终态自行制造恢复动作，也不重写已有取消记录。回归：`tests.test_technical_planning_stage_retry` 的真实 AG-UI 暂停→恢复→确认门，`pnpm test:application-planning-recovery`、`pnpm test:application-planning-runtime`。

二次修改正式修订的模板恢复实测见 `docs/ELECTRON_SECOND_MODIFICATION_RETRY_TEST_2026_10_08.md`。`execution_failure_classifier.py` 在模块判断前处理 WorkspaceBootstrapError：引擎失败为 template_engine 外部依赖，模板配置、包及运行校验失败为 template 业务错误，均保留原 Graph 重试属性。规划 Native Recovery 的 AG-UI 终帧保留节点最新 lifecycle，不用 admission 快照覆盖 continuation_ready；`applicationPlanningWorkflowState.ts` 以明确的 template_reconcile 节点优先于旧技术规划 lifecycle 展示模板阶段。用户显式重试对账到已完成且已签发 continuation 的当前规划时，`applicationPlanningRuntime.ts` 交回原续接，不重放模板或确认。`AiChatPanel` 的当前规划失败入口优先于模板启动遗留的预览重试；独立预览与普通二次修改恢复规则保持原归属。

技术规划中断与失败统一由底部恢复区域展示原因和后端签发的继续/重试动作；`MessageList` 不再展示“技术规划已中断”卡片，空消息整体隐藏，当前失败技术规划消息即使保留交接回执也不显示原 Agent 头像和名称。运行中的 `PlanningWorkflowActivity` 继续展示原进度。同一 Run 的 TemplateEngineError 仍只由底部 Incident 显示“模板生成失败 / 模板更新失败”和原始原因。`execution_failure_classifier.py` 在模块归因前将 TemplateEngineError 识别为 template_engine 外部依赖，避免 workspace_bootstrap.models 被当作模型 SDK；保留稳定错误码、精确 Graph 节点和重放属性。`recoveryFailureMessage.ts` 按当前结构化 dependency/异常类型读取模板原因，`recoveryIncident.ts` 仅修改标题，不改变 Backend 签发动作。普通失败仍由原底部控制面承载，运行中仍显示原 loading，历史和其他执行不受影响。不创建新卡片、不提交恢复动作。回归包含 `tests.test_execution_failure_classifier`、`Frontend/tests/executionRecoveryCard.test.tsx` 与 `Frontend/tests/planningProgressCard.test.ts`。

技术规划原加载卡的运行状态由 `protocols/workflow/projection.py::_workflow_progress_summary` 投影：`workflow.node.completed` 仅结束单个节点，业务增量仍为 `running` 时不能投影整轮 completed。当前 `workflow/definition.py` 维护 TechnicalPlan 事务子节点名称和固定后继，`_workflow_next_nodes` 按候选与确认结果展示真实后继；不改变 Graph 路由、审阅确认或恢复动作。回归：`tests.test_planning_progress_projection`、`tests.test_workflow_ag_ui`、`tests.test_technical_planning_recovery_journey`、`pnpm test:workflow-preview`（含原 MessageList 加载卡渲染）。

正式修订 DEVELOPMENT → DESIGN 交接后，`applicationPlanningRuntime.ts` 不把 transport/只读对账断线视为未入场证明；已匹配当前修订或状态未知时保留目标 StageSession 和来源回执。`AiChatPanel/hooks/useFormalRevisionHandoffRecovery.ts` 按当前 lifecycle 的 change/impact/source/planning 身份定位设计阶段；已有会话复用，展示会话缺失时仅在原 DEVELOPMENT 会话精确存在且当前修订已批准的条件下补当前 StageSession 和回执，原 Graph/Run/checkpoint 不变。不发起新修订、不重做意图识别、不补建开发会话。来源 impact 确认按 active revision 的来源 Run/Thread 失效。回归：`pnpm test:application-planning-runtime`（包含 `formalRevisionHandoffRecovery.test.ts`）、`pnpm test:connection-recovery`。

二次修改同 thread 的新请求显式清空上一轮 clarification/intent/response/result；确认续接与 Native Recovery 仍读取各自原节点状态。`WorkflowRunCard/workflowClarification.ts` 对 conversation 仅在当前轮 `requires_user_input` 时渲染确认，扫描/分类的临时 completed/running 状态不能带出旧表单。

工作台底部 `AiChatPanel/recoverySurface.tsx` 与聊天区 `AiChatPanel.tsx` 全局错误兜底共用二次修改错误展示；`recoveryIncident.ts::isDirectModificationScopeDenied` 按结构化错误码或拒绝文案识别越权，显示“无权修改该路径”并隐藏无效重试，`AgentErrorCard` 说明请求已在执行前拒绝且未写入。其他二次修改失败仍沿用原恢复按钮；durable Incident 仅提供错误与动作证据，不另展示“继续执行”恢复卡。仍先 Attach/Get 再执行后端当前签发动作，并保留 requiresConfirmation 和运行/禁用门禁。断线继续由 `ConnectionStatusBanner` 展示；Build 及其他 Workflow 节点保留原恢复卡；Planning 原确认与恢复控制面保持现有归属。

开发阶段二次修改主动暂停沿现有取消控制等待 `WorkerStateGraph` 同步节点、Agent 与命令真实退出；`protocols/direct_modification.py` 将运行身份绑定到 `run_workers`，SmallTask 未携带 packet 身份时使用该上下文封锁迟到工具。前端原流结束必须等待独立暂停回执，随后仅展示继续控制。`forwardedProps.conversation.resumeExecutionRunId` 只接受当前会话主动暂停的 CANCELLED/STOPPED 来源，保留同 thread 服务端 checkpoint，以 LangGraph Command 仅更新运行元数据并续跑 pending 节点，不重发业务输入、不重新扫描分类、不消费确认门；异常 FAILED/INTERRUPTED 继续使用下述兜底恢复。回归：`tests.test_conversation_pause`、`tests.test_conversation_native_recovery`、`pnpm test:workflow-preview`、`pnpm test:connection-recovery`。

二次修改 `/conversation/run` 使用现有 Workbench execution kind 与 `workflow_scope=conversation`，复用同一 Durable Store、Failure/Business/InterruptedTargetResolver、RecoveryActionPlan、claim/handoff/fork 和 `/execution-recovery/execute`；`services/conversation_execution.py` 只适配原协议运行边界。恢复投影与执行按服务端 scope 选择原 Graph，统一准备后的 child 交回原 Conversation AG-UI 流；首业务节点前提交 `workflow_entry`。前端底部二次修改故障优先统一恢复，预览断线不抢入口；AG-UI 流缺少 RUN_FINISHED/RUN_ERROR 时按通信中断处理。原确认门、修复额度、Formal Revision impact 交接保持原语义，不添加历史 thread 读取或迁移。回归：`tests.test_conversation_native_recovery`、`pnpm test:chat-skills`、`pnpm test:connection-recovery`。

`Frontend/src/renderer/src/service/workbenchProgressMerge.ts` 只合并当前 Run/Thread/会话及更新时间匹配的 GET 进度，保留较高 DAG revision，执行移除后清除旧进度；没有实际变化时返回原生命周期对象，避免影响普通设计/计划页面的渲染和会话恢复。`latestApplicationLifecycle` 的持久 revision、门禁与恢复候选合并规则不变。回归：`pnpm test:connection-recovery`、`pnpm test:development-artifacts`、`pnpm test:dag-generation`。

单测生成器 `Backend/app/agents/test_generation/generator.py` 的正式工件变更快照排除 `.devagentstudio/recovery/execution-recovery.sqlite` 及 SQLite 自有 `-wal`、`-shm`、`-journal` 附属文件，并由 `agents/test_generation/runtime_artifacts.py` 精确排除平台预览的前后端 PID、当前启动器日志、`runtime/launch/preview-runtime.json`、同目录 `.preview-<32位小写十六进制 UUID>.tmp`、`runtime/preview-maintenance.json` 及其 `.tmp` 原子写入文件，避免正常 Graph 租约心跳和并行预览启动/刷新/清理被归因为 Agent 写入。排除范围限定为上述文件；`runtime/launch/`、`runtime/`、`recovery/` 下的其他文件、正式计划、源码和敏感配置仍检查，Agent 文件工具仍只能写测试目录。回归：`tests.test_test_generation_preview_writes`（独立预览线程与真实状态写入、运行文件新增/刷新/清理、临时文件及拒绝 Agent 写入）、`tests.test_test_generation_recovery_writes`、`tests.test_test_generation`。

底部 `retryCurrentRecovery` 必须 await 恢复调用再释放请求锁/恢复身份；本地 Native Recovery 暂停使用当前 AG-UI Workflow 报告的子 Run/Graph thread，避免 lifecycle 迟到时取消源 Run或落入未登记的普通聊天 transport。无本地流时仍用当前会话 lifecycle 归属；普通本地 Workflow 停止路径不变。回归包含 `workflowConversationRuntime.test.ts` 的恢复异步流防重入用例，以及 Electron 报告修复后 E05 实测。

普通本地暂停由 `useWorkflowConversation.handleStopGenerating` 返回实际停止 Promise；失败撤回乐观暂停态，交由 `AiChatPanel.handleStopCurrentGeneration` 即时提示，不写入任务失败入口。`AgUiChatSession` 仅在 Backend 确认停止后强制收口悬挂请求，丢弃旧流迟到事件；未确认停止仍阻止同线程新请求。回归：`pnpm test:chat-skills`、`pnpm test:connection-recovery`，Electron 验收需覆盖运行中 Build、取消失败后再次暂停与暂停后新请求。

`services/workflow_failure_message.py` 只为当前失败 Build 提取已有 `build_summary.finalization_error`，供 lifecycle 错误边界、失败收尾与最终 AG-UI 摘要一致展示；不改变 Build 调度或模板投影。`useWorkflowConversation.handleStopPlan` 对本地恢复流和当前会话冷重入的运行，以 lifecycle 精确 Run/Graph thread 经既有 `/workflow/run` AG-UI cancellation 取消并同步；普通本地 Workflow 停止和待确认计划 Stop 保留原路径，不通过改写 lifecycle 假装停止。回归：`tests.test_workflow_failure_message`、`tests.test_workflow_run_control`、`pnpm test:connection-recovery`。

`useWorkflowConversation.handleEndPlan` 在成功且身份仍匹配的结束回执后清理该会话恢复错误；`globalFallbackState` 对已结束执行排除旧 recoveryError，但连接、投影读取及独立业务错误继续展示。验收预览仅在没有全局兜底时使用 `acceptance-preview-focus`；故障时保留原分栏和底部入口，健康后恢复全宽，不改变验收决定与恢复动作。回归：`pnpm test:connection-recovery`；Electron 实测见恢复验收报告 E06/E07。

`AiChatPanel/developmentNextStep.ts` 只按当前正式 Workflow/lifecycle 的匹配初次完成事实或成功 End 回执生成下一步；`components/NextDevelopmentGuide/` 在 `MessageList` 末尾提供推荐任务和可搜索的页面/API 清单，沿用明暗主题变量。点击后复用当前 DEVELOPMENT 可见会话、追加任务消息并创建独立 Graph thread，通过原 `/workflow/run` 和所有开发确认门。会话创建时的目标元数据不改写；最新正式运行的显式目标驱动后续请求和重开会话的详情上下文。暂停、失败、普通节点完成与失败 End 不显示引导，新正式任务取代旧引导。回归：`pnpm test:development-artifacts`、`pnpm test:connection-recovery`。

UI 生成池中断由 `services/ui_design_recovery.py` 在当前 `ui_designs` Native Interrupt 的只读对账中识别：磁盘 queued/generating 且当前池不持有、正式 ProductPlan 仍持有的页面，投影为 `applicationPlanningRecovery.uiGenerationRecovery.pageIds`。健康连接不能清掉这个底部重试；显式重试先重新 GET，再提交当前审阅门的 `ui_action/refresh`，进入 `ui_confirmation` 已有 `_latest_ui_designs` 自愈机制。活跃任务、已完成页和已确认产物不重做，不推进下游；只读查询不入队。回归：`tests.test_ui_design_recovery`、`pnpm test:application-planning-runtime`。

底部连接从 unavailable 恢复 healthy 时，规划视图通过 `AppEntryPage → WorkbenchPage → LeftPanel → AiChatPanel` 的 `onReconcilePlanning` 调用既有 Runtime single-flight AG-UI 只读对账；包括预览订阅发现的后端断线，不增加轮询或自动派发节点恢复。`useWorkflowConversation.retryCurrentRecovery` 同步到同会话正常 running/awaiting_user 执行时不制造 NO_RECOVERY_ENTRY_ERROR；当前会话正常 completed 回执清理该合成兜底错误，独立业务失败与其它会话不受影响。回归：`pnpm test:connection-recovery`，Electron UI 生成后停服与待验收重连。

Recovery GET 投影增加可选 `error`（`RECOVERY_PROJECTION_READ_FAILED`），候选列表查询、单条解析或外层投影异常均明确报告读取失败。Frontend 合并时保留仍被 lifecycle 持有的旧候选证据，但读取失败禁止执行旧动作；即使连接健康或旧状态仍 running，也显示底部同步重试。只有成功的空投影或 End 删除执行才能清除候选；原生重入、确认门和节点内部修复机制不变。

`AiChatPanel/pendingWorkflowInput.ts` 与 `hooks/useWorkflowConversation.ts` 保留普通发送在 Run 建立前失败的内存输入快照。底部重试仍先走既有 lifecycle Attach/Get；有当前 Recovery 时沿用原后端动作，无候选且无同会话新 execution 时只恢复原输入和技能。用户再次明确发送时沿用冻结的页面/Endpoint/实体目标及原 AG-UI 入口。空候选不被视为请求未接收的证明，不自动重发；读取失败、切换会话、新执行事实或输入区的新内容均阻止错误恢复。确认、续接 token、调试、主动停止和认证失败不进入此输入恢复分支。不增加持久化字段或后端重试合同。回归：`pnpm test:connection-recovery` 的 Workflow Conversation Runtime 测试。

集成测试 `integration_test` 与集成局部修复 `small_task_repair` 的业务失败及异常失败，底部统一签发从 `integration_test` 开始的“重新测试”。`domain/workflow_recovery_boundary.py::failed_reentry_target` 是解析、恢复事务与持久化校验共用的失败恢复边界；真实失败节点不改写。必须找到同一 Run 的测试入口 checkpoint，缺失时明确拒绝，不降级到重试 SmallTask；lifecycle 仍按真实失败节点证明身份。恢复事务保留失败修复调用的合法代码变更证据，但不复用其旧任务。回归：`tests.test_integration_native_recovery`、`tests.test_workflow_reentry_contract`。

验收 `acceptance` 的项目启动失败通过现有 BUSINESS_RETRY 重入同一验收节点，仍校验 lifecycle、Run/Thread、checkpoint 与资源归属。启动节点显式写入本轮 `message/error`，成功时清空旧错误；AG-UI 下一节点投影仅在明确验收通过后宣告 `finalize_project`，失败或待用户验收时不得覆盖 durable 当前节点。预览底栏的“验收通过”调用 `useWorkflowConversation.handleAcceptPreview`，沿 `/workflow/run` 提交 `page_acceptance: accepted`，不再本地乐观改写通过状态；提交期间按钮禁用并显示 loading。`protocols/workflow/lifecycle.py` 在验收节点实际确认后将质量门结果与 execution 同次落盘，原 `finalize_project` 释放 execution 和资源锁，前端消费服务端快照。未改 Agent 节点或增加 Lifecycle 校验。回归：`tests.test_acceptance_decision_projection`、`tests.test_workflow_failure_message`、`tests.test_workflow_reentry_contract`。

所有阶段统一取消 checkpoint 旧工作区 revision/hash 的恢复门禁及其快照文件存在性要求；`execution_recovery_executor.py` 只保留运行身份、精确节点入口、正式上下文和 lifecycle/资源归属校验。没有逐节点豁免名单或旧门禁失败的特殊重试分支。工作区指纹仍用于缓存和业务证据有效性：Build、测试、审查保留各自重入刷新；`prepare_build_tasks` 恢复先扫描当前磁盘再冻结规划输入；`acceptance` 恢复重新走确定性启动器检查当前项目，不能仅凭旧 preview URL 跳过启动。用户已提交的验收决定仍按原确认合同消费，不重新运行验收。回归包括 `tests.test_execution_recovery_langgraph_native`、`tests.test_recovery_current_workspace` 与 `tests.test_acceptance_subgraph`。

代码审查 `code_review` 同样不以旧工作区 revision/snapshot hash 阻止 Native Recovery；`services/code_review_reentry.py` 仅在首次已验证重入中重新扫描当前源码，保留 Diff/full 方式、原入口的修复确认、同范围已提交问题快照和修复轮次，再进入原审查子图。Diff 文件范围由现有服务端正式计划重新选取，正常扫描/修复及用户确认门不变；审查支持同一 INTERRUPTED_CONTINUE、FAILURE_RETRY 和经 lifecycle 证明的 BUSINESS_RETRY 入口，运行身份、checkpoint、资源归属与 Build 绑定仍严格校验。回归：`tests.test_code_review_reentry`、`tests.test_code_review_native_recovery`。

测试阶段 `services/integration_test_reentry.py` 在经验证的首次失败重入中刷新工作区、清空旧检查/报告/修复任务/交接与性能选择，并把本轮 `repair_iteration` 重置为 0；保留单测决定、既有代码变更归属与修复上限。入口更新先合并，再由真实节点结果覆盖，避免旧 checkpoint 状态残留或入口重置覆盖新报告。暂停续接保留原修复额度与同入口任务进度，正常内部修复循环不重置。执行归属、资源锁和 checkpoint 校验保持不变，已执行或起点变化的事务禁止再次准备。沿用现有 AG-UI 恢复入口；前端 `service/recoveryIncident.ts` 只解释服务端签发的测试重启，不推断恢复目标。回归：`tests.test_integration_test_reentry`、`tests.test_integration_native_recovery`、`pnpm test:execution-recovery`。

`processStepHistory.ts` 在当前 Workflow 存在 `workflow.run.started` / `workflow.node.started` 时只按本次 Run 的完成事件恢复步骤，不从来源 checkpoint 的整段 timeline 补建完成节点，避免 Electron 重启后继续测试局部修复时展示扫描、DAG、Build、单元测试等开发阶段进度。真实实时步骤、工具活动与本次 Run 的完成事件继续保留；不改变 Native Recovery 执行入口和恢复合同。回归：`pnpm test:chat-skills`。

测试局部修复的 `small_task.tool_activity` 经 `protocols/workflow/runtime.py` 投影为 `agent-process`：调用 ID 包含节点、轮次、任务与 callId，同一调用的开始/完成/失败原位更新，并携带 `attempt`；验证命令使用 command kind。前端 `processStepHistory.ts` 保留 `small_task_repair` / `unit_test_repair` 的工具步骤，`ProcessSteps/processDisplayItems.ts` 按节点轮次归属到修复卡片，调用链默认收起，当前操作与安全文件路径仅在调用链中展示。`RepairInProgressPanel` 只展示 workflow kind 的修复节点状态，修复卡片不展示英文标签，单次 tool/command 调用由 `RepairToolActivityRow` 直接展示状态与主题文件活动，不提供子步骤展开交互。其他正式节点沿用原工具隐藏规则；不改变修复调度、额度、确认或恢复合同。回归：`tests.test_workflow_ag_ui`、`pnpm test:chat-skills`。

单测恢复保留精确的 `unit_test` / `unit_test_repair` 入口。`services/unit_test_reentry.py` 在目标节点首次恢复调用中刷新工作区，不用旧源码指纹阻止测试生成或修复后的重入；保留测试缓存、Build 范围与绑定、已消耗的修复额度，撤销旧通过标记。失败修复进度须匹配入口绑定，已完成 SmallTask 保留，未完成任务恢复 pending，新失败重试重新计费；中断无已提交内部进度时重跑节点入口任务。单测命令重新执行，测试生成器自行验证源码摘要缓存。回归：`tests.test_unit_test_reentry`、`tests.test_unit_test_repair_budget`。

Build 恢复按来源分流：FAILED 重入 `build` 后读取同 Run/Thread/正式计划绑定的内部进度，保留已完成任务，局部重试可重试失败任务或继续已准备好的 Build 修复计划；INTERRUPTED 和显式从 Build 节点执行则由 `services/build_restart.py` 清空旧进度，按 Formal DAG 重建 pending 清单并创建独立 Build Run。两条路径都重新扫描当前工作区，不用旧源码指纹阻止重入；Agent 根据真实文件判断 `already_satisfied`，平台照常验收。`services/execution_recovery_executor.py` 保留执行身份、checkpoint、lifecycle ownership 校验；数据库审批等正常交互确认续行保持原行为。回归：`tests.test_native_build_entry_recovery`、`tests.test_build_subgraph_scheduler`。

DAG 中断候选由 `Backend/app/workspace/planning_interruption_documents.py` 保存到 `.devagentstudio/runtime/planning-interruption/<workflow_run_id>.json`（`planning-interruption.v1`）。Controller 每次提交后尽力写入当前有效候选和 revision；Global Repair 重开的候选立即排除，failed/cancelled Run 不保留中断候选。普通生成和 Regenerate 的 `INTERRUPTED_CONTINUE` 按后端指定的 source Run 读取证据，验证 owner、thread、PlanningRun 和当前磁盘投影 revision 后，再经过既有输入指纹、正式基线摘要、Scope、Local Validator 和 Global 验证；缺失、损坏、失效或写入失败只放弃复用，不改变 Local Retry / Global Repair 预算、失败分类和 Pending/Formal 确认门。原有失败 Recovery Snapshot 保持独立；中断证据不伪造 failed Run，也不授权恢复。Native checkpoint admission 仍按现有合同执行，工作区变化只影响候选/缓存是否可复用，不阻止恢复。

`READY_NATIVE` requires both a replay-safe business boundary and a fork-stable Graph boundary. Fork stability means that changing runtime ownership fields through LangGraph `update_state`/`aupdate_state` preserves the exact successor set. A boundary whose successor exists only in an ephemeral `Command(goto=...)` is not production-native-recoverable. The current root LangGraph checkpoint `values.active_run_id` is the authority for the active normal execution component; DurableExecution supplies status/evidence only, while RecoveryAttempt supplies recovery-child edges. User-facing projection and execution paths read that checkpoint identity before resolving lineage, so older AWAITING_USER normal resumes cannot create false multiple leaves and stale actions cannot self-authorize. The current `application-planning.requirement-answer.v1` boundary persists `application_planning_review_route` in internal Graph State and resolves `requirements_review` through a fail-closed conditional edge; the TechnicalPlan transaction uses the same durable routing rule plus five backend-owned recovery boundaries, including `REVIEW_READY` for generation failures before the review interrupt. These route, candidate, and recovery-boundary authorities are excluded from public Workflow projections.

| Path                                | Responsibility                                                                                                                                             | Read first                                                                                                                        |
| ----------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `AGENTS.md`                         | Repository-wide agent instructions, validation rules, and architecture constraints.                                                                        | Always read before edits.                                                                                                         |
| `docs/CODEBASE_INDEX.md`            | Directory/function routing map for future agents.                                                                                                          | Read before broad search or planning.                                                                                             |
| `contracts/authorization-api.v1.yaml` | 权限管理前后端共享的本地 OpenAPI 唯一事实源；启用 authorization capability 的后端模板副本必须与其 SHA-256 一致，前端只生成类型并通过既有 `service.ts` 发起请求。 | Read before changing authorization management APIs, auth-template generation, generated authorization types, or service wrappers. |
| `docs/BOOTSTRAP_PLAN.md` | Bootstrap、TemplateState、Readiness、通用 Route Projection、Build 和 Workspace Attach 的统一重构契约；Materializer 对通过 ZIP 安全校验的模板文件执行通用完整物化并校验相对路径与 SHA-256，不为模板内部文件维护 allow-list；验收状态以实际证据为准。 | Read first for template generation, readiness, projection ownership, or interrupted Workspace recovery. |
| `docs/WORKFLOW.md`                  | Design notes for the LangGraph workflow that coordinates requirement confirmation, project planning, code generation, integration testing, and acceptance. | Read before changing workflow phases, agent boundaries, Graph State shape, build/test subgraphs, or context/persistence strategy. |
| `docs/DAG_TASK_GENERATION_AND_VALIDATION_MININAL_CHANGE_PLAN.md` | Current minimal-change contract for Build DAG generation, deterministic validation, separate Pending/Formal authority, Confirm/Abandon/Regenerate actions, application-level Planning exclusivity, and the boundary between template initialization, runtime context, and Build execution. | Read before changing task preparation, DAG validation, Build gates, task-confirmation actions, Planning cancellation/refresh, or menu/route/page-placeholder ownership. |
| `docs/DAG_TASK_GENERATION_UNIT_CANDIDATE_PLAN.md` | Target architecture for refactoring Scope-level combined task generation into current-PlanningRun UnitCandidate generation, local validation/retry, explicit-source Recovery Snapshot reuse, confirmed-DAG-based reuse, serialized Scope assembly, and attributable global validation. Candidate current identity (`CandidateIdentity`) is separate from generated/recovered provenance; failed Runs remain terminal, Retry creates a new Run, and Recovery is never Pending/Formal authority. | Read after the minimal-change plan and before designing or implementing PlanningRun state, per-Unit model requests, candidate persistence, reuse facts, progress events, local retry orchestration, or Recovery consumption. |
| `docs/APPLICATION_REVISION_WORKFLOW.md` | Canonical design for post-creation Change lifecycle: stable change identity, target-aware impact analysis, candidate artifacts, confirmation gates, invalidation closure, Build DAG execution, acceptance, promotion, rollback, resource locking, and AG-UI projection. | Read first before changing secondary modification, acceptance adjustment, revision events/cards, candidate promotion, or quick-modification completion semantics. |
| `docs/APPLICATION_DEVELOPMENT_PLANNING.md` | Workbench page/API selection, Endpoint API-design/readiness gating, retained independent legacy EntitySourceBinding, Build DAG confirmation, and numbered development-task planning. | Read before changing workbench target selection, API-design readiness, development planning, or `menus.developmentTasks`. |
| `docs/FIELD_VALUE_RULES.md` | Current single-source value-rule UI and v7 storage contract, dependencies, missing policies, and downstream generation/acceptance behavior. | Read before changing query/write/external-request or response value configuration. |
| `docs/PRODUCT_UI_TECHNICAL_PLANNING.md` | Canonical creation/design boundary: RequirementSpec → ProductPlan → React UiDesign → explicit planning-stage entry → TechnicalPlan → per-Endpoint API Design, including PageImplementationContract, field-source snapshots, retained legacy EntitySourceBinding boundary, and 128k context budget. | Read first when changing planning phases, ProductPlan/TechnicalPlan storage, UI authority, page implementation contracts, API design, or Endpoint semantics. |
| `docs/DESIGN_CONVERSATION_AGENT.md` | Canonical design for the bottom Design Conversation Agent: earliest-node RequirementSpec/ProductPlan/UiDesign routing, incremental revision, live regeneration projection, confirmation gates, and the hard stop before TechnicalPlan. | Read first when changing design-phase bottom chat, design artifact rollback/invalidation, regeneration status UI, or the independent design conversation AG-UI endpoint. |
| `scripts/start-backend.sh`          | Standard macOS/POSIX backend dev-server command for agents and humans.                                                                                       | Run this on macOS when asked to start the backend service.                                                                        |
| `scripts/start-backend.ps1`         | Standard Windows PowerShell backend dev-server command with Python 3.12 virtual-environment discovery.                                                       | Run this on Windows when asked to start the backend service.                                                                      |
| `scripts/build-backend-mac.sh`      | macOS PyInstaller build and architecture-specific staging script for the packaged backend executable, required built-in skills, page templates, and the selected `.env[.st|.uat|.prd]`; requires `--env` and accepts Python 3.12 or 3.14. | Run with `x64` on Intel or `arm64` on Apple Silicon before matching `Frontend` mac packaging; staging stops on architecture or bundled-resource validation failures. |
| `docs/CROSS_PLATFORM_SUPPORT.md`    | Supported Windows/macOS architectures, development commands, packaging flow, signing boundary, and reference-architecture mapping.                          | Read before changing platform adapters, process lifecycle, or desktop packaging.                                                  |
| `scripts/build-backend-win.ps1`     | Windows-only PyInstaller build and staging script for the packaged backend exe, required built-in skills, page templates, and the selected `.env[.st|.uat|.prd]`; requires `-Env` and defaults to Python 3.12 and accepts Python 3.14 through `-Python`. | Run on a Windows CI/VM before `Frontend` Windows packaging; staging stops when bundled resource validation fails.                    |
| `scripts/mac_pack_arm64.sh`, `scripts/mac_pack_x64.sh` | One-command macOS development package entries that build/stage the backend before packaging Electron for the matching host architecture; `--slim` limits bundled grammars. | Run the entry matching the Mac CPU from the repository root; outputs are under `Frontend/dist`. |
| `Frontend/electron-builder.yml` | Default macOS/Windows Electron packaging config: allowlists built `out`, app metadata, and icon while excluding frontend `node_modules`; stages the matching backend via `extraResources`. | Read before changing desktop package contents or size; validate both the packaged app and backend resource after changes. |
| `scripts/win_pack.ps1`              | One-command Windows x64 development package entry that builds/stages the backend before packaging Electron; forwards the optional `-Python` interpreter and `-Slim` grammar profile. | Run from the repository root on Windows; output is under `Frontend/dist`. |
| `Backend/`                          | FastAPI backend, agent runtime, planning/orchestration, and local workspace tools.                                                                          | `Backend/app/main.py`, then the relevant module below.                                                                            |
| `Frontend/`                         | Electron + React desktop client with Vite, Ant Design, local session/app storage, AG-UI frontend clients.                                                  | `Frontend/src/main/index.ts`, `Frontend/src/renderer/src/pages/AppEntryPage.tsx`.                                                 |

## Backend

验收拒绝沿用 `/workflow/run` 的 AG-UI End：`AiChatPanel.handleAcceptanceReject` 等待 `useWorkflowConversation.handleEndPlan` 的明确成功结果后才创建开发会话；失败保留原验收入口。`handleEndPlan` 返回 boolean 供顺序交接，原结束控制仍等待真实工作退出及后端释放，不新增 Lifecycle 校验。Build 统一重试在旧工作退出后创建新 Run，读取原失败上下文及磁盘执行记录，不再创建同 Run 的 checkpoint 恢复尝试或归档降级绑定。回归：`tests.test_checkpoint_first_retry`、`pnpm test:connection-recovery`，并在 Electron 覆盖拒绝验收和 Build 执行中断后单次重试。

质量结果失效由 `services/workflow_quality_results.py` 提供：真实 Build/代码修改入口与正式修订确认把旧测试、审查、验收结果改为 pending，纯只读对话保留结果。`ConversationExecution.started_node` 在平台运行边界更新结果，不改 Agent 节点。前端 `utils.currentRoundAcceptancePassed` 只展示当前无活动执行/正式修改的通过结果；`hooks/revisionSession.revisionStartTarget` 使用 impact 绑定目标，空对象不再遮蔽 application 默认目标。回归：`tests.test_acceptance_decision_projection`、`pnpm test:acceptance-phase`。

- `agents/design_conversation/router.py` 与 `models.py` 共用当前产品对话语义契约（intent/change_level/suggested_phase），由既有 Policy 解析正式节点；不再生产旧 target 路由对象。调用失败使用 `fallback.py` 的保守语义，非法模型对象降级为零写入；回归为 `tests.test_product_conversation_policy`、`tests.test_application_design_conversation`。

- `services/execution_recovery_executor.py` 对 design_intent_analysis 在取得产物 ownership 前的失败，仅在同 thread、同 lifecycle revision、同源 checkpoint Run 的待确认阶段采用 PRE_OWNERSHIP CAS；后续归属校验保持原规则，回归为 `tests.test_execution_recovery_executor`。

- `services/planning_failure_diagnostic.py` 从同 Run/Thread 的失败 PlanningRun 主失败与各轮结构化 Issues 投影简明中文 `userMessage` 和详细 `message`，实时 Workflow AG-UI 与当前 Recovery 查询共用；查询只补充展示，不修改 Durable 失败身份或恢复动作。`ExecutionFailureEvidence.user_message` 与公共 `failureDiagnostic.userMessage` 是可选展示字段；前端 `service/recoveryFailureMessage.ts` 统一展示中文规划说明及明确模型错误码/HTTP 状态映射，技术信息保留在恢复卡详情。

- Unit Candidate 模型调用失败由 `services/unit_model_failure.py` 在 SDK 调用边界分类。六个当前错误码与旧 `UNIT_GENERATION_INFRASTRUCTURE_FAILURE` 共用受限 Planning Recovery 门禁；Snapshot 保留原码及摘要。`services/unit_generation_orchestrator.py` 将分类写入 Controller 主 `PlanningRun.failure`，`protocols/workflow/runtime.py` 仅从同一 Run 的主失败投影到 Workflow summary、AG-UI custom/snapshot、RUN_ERROR 和显式 Durable `ExecutionFailureEvidence`。`services/execution_failure_classifier.py::public_failure_diagnostic` 为实时与刷新提供同一公开字段白名单，包括可选 `stage` 与 `providerErrorCode`；恢复节点仍由 `RecoveryActionPlan` 决定。前端 `service/agUiAgent.ts` 合并同 Run 的晚到通用终帧，`service/recoveryIncident.ts` 只在当前 source Run 的卡片显示可信模型诊断。

- 审查模式：`services/development_review_files.py` 从正式 Build 计划中已完成且仍有未提交目标文件的模块汇总全部 `target_files`，与顶部分支菜单的“已完成模块”同源；`graph/nodes/lifecycle.py` 将清单固定在 checkpoint，`protocols/workflow/runtime.py` 跨阶段传递，审查入口和扫描重试从服务端计划刷新。`agents/code_analyze/{agent,scope,analyzer}.py` 在 Diff 模式下仅允许读取清单内前后端文件（含后端配置与资源）及三个必需 Skill 文件；全量模式沿用原路径。入口与报告分别见 `Frontend/src/renderer/src/components/AiChatPanel/components/WorkflowRunCard/ReviewPhaseConfirmationCard.tsx` 和 `workspace/code_review_documents.py`。

### Initial development completion and test entry

- Initial development registration and continuation phases use the current `api_design_readiness_gate` entry. A retained confirmed API mapping may occupy `WorkflowRunCard` only at that gate; downstream unit-test confirmation must render its skip/run actions. Build completion alone never increments the lifecycle completion count: `test_phase_confirmation` requires the unit-test gate to pass or be explicitly skipped first.
- `Backend/app/domain/development_artifacts.py` defines the strict page/Endpoint/entity identity, initial progress, catalog availability, and derived `TestEntryGate` contracts. `services/development_artifacts.py` owns confirmed ProductPlan/TechnicalPlan catalog reconciliation, sticky first completion, and the single application-wide test-entry rule. The gate counts every current page, Endpoint, and entity even when the current Build plan excludes a target; Build scope only describes the selected execution. Entity progress comes from the current persisted EntitySourceBinding confirmation and active data_source executions. QuickTaskGuide and ApplicationOutline consume this lifecycle status for card text, dots, and counts.
- Iteration origin: every artifact progress record carries `completedBranchName` (the branch/version that first completed it), written once at `complete_initial_development` and inherited verbatim by `completed_development_artifacts`; entity progress re-derives its status each reconcile, so its label is explicitly carried over from the previous record. UI design pages are tracked separately in `.devagentstudio/iteration-artifacts.json` (`services/iteration_artifacts.py`), which is **deliberately absent from `iteration_service._CLEARABLE_DIRS`/`_CLEARABLE_FILES`** — `specs/` and `ui-design/` are wiped on every new iteration, so this file is the only place that can still answer "which iteration designed this page" and "which iteration planned it but never designed it". `graph/nodes/ui_confirmation.py::_persist_ui_designs` is the single write point (covers first entry, per-page regenerate, confirm and skip) and the confirmation payload exposes `iteration_origins`; frontend `service/iterationOrigin.ts` + `components/IterationBadge` render the labels on QuickTaskGuide, ApplicationOutline, DevelopmentArtifactsPanel and the UI design panel.
- `.devagentstudio/application-lifecycle.json.developmentArtifacts` is the sole completion authority. Every lifecycle write reconciles the current confirmed catalog under the existing lock; the AG-UI lifecycle `get` calibrates cold/reconnected reads. `testEntryGate` is projection-only. Execution `developmentPurpose`/`developmentTarget` are server-owned and preserved across initial-development continuations, while revisions never backfill completion. Explicit per-entity skipping uses `/application-lifecycle/run` action `skip_entity_development` with `entityId` and `reason`, implemented in `services/entity_development_skip.py`. It requires the binding execution to have ended and records entity-only `skipped` / `skippedAt` / `skipReason` in the current workspace lifecycle without changing formal plans or inventing completion. Reconciliation preserves this decision, test-entry counts exclude skipped entities, and the UI labels them as skipped using existing theme tokens. Other entities and workspaces remain required; skipped facts are not inherited as completed facts into new iterations.
- Downstream `unit_test`, `unit_test_repair`, and `test_phase_confirmation` lifecycle registration reads the same-thread server checkpoint’s `build_execution_slice.scope`; debug UI defaults cannot replace a completed page/Endpoint Build target with application scope. Runtime keeps Graph and execution targets aligned without replaying reducer fields. For an unfinished target outside a formal revision, that verified Build continuation can establish initial-development purpose; completion still requires Build and unit gates. Regression: `tests.test_development_completion_resume` exercises actual AG-UI/checkpoint flows and 1/2 counts.
- `graph/nodes/lifecycle.py::test_phase_confirmation` records page/Endpoint targets only after Build and the development unit-test gate pass, before checking all targets. When an independently confirmed entity is the final artifact, `graph/nodes/planning.py::entity_source_binding` projects the same confirmation card; `protocols/workflow/runtime.py` carries its server checkpoint identity across the test-session handoff, and the confirmation node validates the entity's formal completion without inventing a Build Unit. Workflow lifecycle startup and `graph/subgraphs/testing.py::integration_test` recheck the same gate; test interaction consumption and execution replacement share one atomic write. Blocked runs emit a structured `development_artifacts_incomplete` result and finish the AG-UI lifecycle without marking development failed.
- Frontend `developmentArtifacts.ts`, `context/WorkbenchPhaseContext.tsx` and `context/workbenchPhaseState.ts` own shared phase access and live gate consumption. `ApplicationOutline/DevelopmentStatusDot` and `ApiOutlineGroup` render initial status and unfiltered counts; `WorkbenchTopBar` and historical `TestPhaseConfirmationCard` consume the same application lifecycle. Test, review, and acceptance views remain blocked while the application-wide gate is closed, including previously reached views. Test browsing creates no session; actual confirmation changes phase only after server execution registration, and an unaccepted test session is discarded.
- `AiChatPanel/workflowDebugResume.ts` binds downstream node debugging to the most recent Build thread in the current conversation that is still registered in lifecycle. Both send-button debugging and paused-plan debugging resume the execution, never the chat thread. An explicitly selected debug node may atomically replace an `awaiting_user` execution and skip its pending interaction; ordinary resume cannot, and `running`/`stopping` executions remain protected. Server checkpoint validation remains authoritative; a newer failed Build cannot fall back to an earlier success. Regression coverage is in `Frontend/tests/workflowPreview.test.ts` and `Backend/tests/test_execution_resource_locks.py`.
- 成功 End 后的显式节点调试新建 Workflow Run，不提交控制回执或已结束执行的 `resumeExecutionRunId`，也不提交旧 `resumeState`；`workflowDebugResume.ts` 按 End 回执的精确 `targetRunId` 选择原业务 Graph thread；当前会话的连续入场前拒绝不会覆盖该 End 边界，遇到已入场节点事件或仍登记的执行便停止回溯。调试续接令牌只来自当前 lifecycle 按 Run/thread 精确匹配的执行，不能使用消息 Run ID 或其他页面/应用执行；显式新调试清理旧恢复错误。暂停/失败/待确认执行的调试续接仍保留原令牌与后端校验。回归：`pnpm test:connection-recovery`（真实 End → integration_test 调试请求）、`pnpm test:workflow-preview`。
- `AiChatPanel/components/NextDevelopmentGuide` owns the post-completion/End guide and full page/API picker; `pickerTasks.ts` jointly filters status/type/search and suppresses the guide only after a successful nonempty catalog read with every page/Endpoint completed. Loading, catalog errors and unknown progress are not completion. Existing test confirmation keeps sole ownership of the all-completed next step.
- Regression entry points: Backend `tests.test_development_artifacts`, `tests.test_workflow_test_phase_confirmation`, lifecycle/continuation stream tests; Frontend `pnpm test:development-artifacts`, existing workflow/stage tests, targeted ESLint and `pnpm build`. Runtime UI verification must use Electron.
- `ApplicationOutline/DevelopmentStatusDot.less` owns the purple in-progress pulse (with reduced-motion support); `styles/global.less` defines dedicated light/dark completion greens. `WorkflowRunCard/TestPhaseConfirmationCard.less` owns the themed confirmation layout: blocked cards list each remaining artifact with its type and count, while test target and action appear only after the live gate allows entry.
- `Frontend/src/renderer/src/workbenchPhaseNavigation.ts` owns the shared phase order and per-application furthest-reached browsing record (`devagentstudio:workbench-reached-phase:<applicationId>`). `WorkbenchPhaseContext` keeps this separate from the current view and active execution; `useChatSessions` restores browsing reach from the current nonempty stage-session directory after its stale-load check. `WorkbenchTopBar` uses this record for revisiting stages, while test entry still obeys the live server gate. Application deletion clears the record. Regression coverage lives in `Frontend/tests/workbenchPhaseNavigation.test.tsx`, included by `pnpm test:development-artifacts`.

```text
Backend/app/
├── main.py
├── config.py
├── graph/          # LangGraph workflow lifecycle, state, nodes, and subgraphs
├── agents/         # First-class Deep Agent runtimes plus the read-only change-impact analyzer
├── domain/         # Framework-independent core data models and workflow contracts
├── services/       # Deterministic business rules, normalization, scheduling, and quality gates
├── tools/          # Controlled tools exposed to Deep Agents
├── protocols/      # Protocol integration
├── workspace/      # Project workspace documents and sandboxed local file/tool access
├── middleware/     # Cross-cutting Deep Agent controls
├── persistence/    # Business and runtime persistence
├── observability/  # Runtime events and observability
└── utils/          # Shared dependency-light parsing and utility helpers
```

| Path                                                    | Responsibility                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Read first / common edits                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `Backend/app/main.py`                                   | FastAPI app setup and route registration for `/health`, primary `/workflow/run`, Backend-owned recovery `/execution-recovery/execute`, optionally page/API-targeted `/conversation/run`, independent read-only `/temporary-chat/run`, standalone product AG-UI actions, Electron-only deletion preparation `/application-deletion/prepare`, the dedicated data-source router, controlled `/tools/*` endpoints (including database metadata), approvals, git/workspace tools, and independent AG-UI preview controls `/preview-runtime/run`. Its lifespan clears main, creation-planning, and conversation Graph caches before closing shared checkpoint storage. | Add or change shared HTTP endpoints here; data-source HTTP changes belong in `app/routes/data_sources.py`. |
| `Backend/app/config.py`                                 | Environment-backed model provider, endpoint, credentials, and runtime settings, including `BUILD_TASK_PLAN_MAX_RETRIES` (default `2`) for platform-owned Build DAG candidate regeneration and `DEVAGENTSTUDIO_DAG_BUSINESS_SELF_CHECK_ENABLED` (default `false`) for Build-stage business self-check execution.                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Read when changing `.env` variables, model/client configuration, DAG generation retry policy, or Build business self-check behavior.                                                                                                                    |
| `Backend/app/services/workspace_bootstrap/`, `Backend/app/services/template_state.py`, `Backend/app/services/template_reconcile/`, `project_launcher.py` | First Generate keeps the confirmed TechnicalPlan and V3 project ZIP. Existing formal business revisions and the separate configuration capability path both use V4 `/v1/update` Capability Ensure: it compares canonical `application.json` Desired with `template-state.json` Applied, returns local `NO_CHANGE` without an Engine Client when equal, and uses capability-only RequestedConfig, stateSequence, size-only payloads and no SHA/digest only when a capability is missing or mismatched. Legacy `managedConfiguration` is read but excluded from V4 request and next State. `ApplicationParameterUpdater` owns `.env` and YAML parameters instead of the Engine Update. Bootstrap still accepts only the specified project roots and `.devagentstudio/template-contracts/` from Generate. | Read before changing Template Engine configuration, Bootstrap/update contracts, V3/V4 State, first-workspace initialization, or generated application parameters. |
| `Backend/app/services/application_config_orchestrator.py`, `application_parameter_updater/`, `protocols/application_config_operation.py`, `services/application_config_prerequisite.py` | Independent `/application-config/run` AG-UI preview/apply/get/resume contract merges only named configuration fields with canonical application.json, checks configRevision and installed capabilities, persists non-sensitive operation status, calls V4 only for missing capabilities, and updates exact dotenv/YAML leaves. `application_config_prerequisite` deterministically recognizes application-level theme/icon/name/login/authorization intent before direct code classification; same-value pure configuration completes without an Agent, while mixed requests preserve the original request and classify only the remaining business intent. Parameter writes use a `0600` short-lived before/after journal and recover safely after interruption. Direct conversation pauses at an explicit configuration card, then resumes the same checkpoint only after its request-bound operation is applied. Formal Revision has no configuration Delta path. The settings and conversation clients use this route; the Electron write IPC rejects bypasses for generated applications' managed config fields. | Read with `services/application_config/`, `template_reconcile/`, frontend `service/applicationConfigOperation.ts`, `SettingsPage/`, `WorkflowRunCard/`, and Electron `main/index.ts` when changing config transaction, field mapping, or failure recovery. |
| `Backend/packaging/devagentstudio-backend.spec`             | PyInstaller onedir definition that bundles the Backend application, built-in skills under `_internal/app/builtin_skills`, frontend page-template source under `_internal/app/page_templates`, package data, and hidden imports; the optional built-in grammar profile excludes custom-only Tree-sitter bindings. | Read when changing packaged Python/data dependencies, grammar support, or frozen resource layout. |
| `Backend/packaging/verify_bundled_skills.py`            | Cross-platform post-PyInstaller validator used by both Backend staging scripts to reject bundles missing built-in skills or page templates, and to compare packaged templates with frontend source. | Update with required skill/template files or supported PyInstaller resource layouts. |
| `Backend/packaging/backend_server.py`                   | Frozen Backend entry point: loads packaged configuration, validates required built-in skills before Uvicorn starts, and launches the API server.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Read when changing packaged Backend startup, frozen resource discovery, or fail-fast requirements.                                                                                                                  |
| `Backend/app/graph/workflow.py`                         | Primary LangGraph workflow declaration: page/API development enters `api_design_readiness_gate`, which checks current Endpoint API Design versions before `inspect_workspace -> prepare_build_tasks`. Production task planning uses the async Planning adapter and stops at a PendingPlan confirmation gate; exact Confirm promotes Formal and resumes Build, Regenerate consumes the old Pending and creates a new PlanningRun, and Abandon ends the matching execution. Legacy EntitySourceBinding nodes remain registered only as an independent flow. Later phases retain explicit test/review/acceptance gates and failures route to `handle_failure`. | Read when adding, removing, renaming, rerouting workflow phases, changing workflow checkpoint behavior, or selecting planning authority. Keep node logic in `graph/nodes/` or subgraphs. |
| `Backend/app/graph/direct_modification_workflow.py` | Independent optionally targeted conversation Graph: bounded read-only workspace scan and Q&A, intent classification, direct local implementation fixes owned by frontend/backend/fullstack, optional pre-execution `implementation_fix_confirmation` controlled by `confirmScopeBeforeExecution` (default on), pre-execution rejection for paths outside the current owner's writable directory, confirmed SmallTask scope expansions, or formal Workflow handoff. Requests without a concrete desired change enter clarification even when scope confirmation is off. No separate workspace-write Agent/node exists. Backend-first fullstack execution is followed by `validate_direct_fix`: affected frontend changes run mandatory tsc/Vite checks before the existing project restart validation, without unit tests. Successful changes finalize without entering formal acceptance; the bounded repair loop returns to the same validation. Checkpoints use the StageSession thread; `services/conversation_execution.py` connects scope `conversation` to Workbench lifecycle, DurableExecution, leases and shared recovery. | Read with `graph/nodes/direct_modification.py`, `services/direct_validation.py`, `graph/nodes/direct_repair.py`, `agents/direct_modification.py`, and `protocols/direct_modification.py`. |
| `Backend/app/graph/nodes/direct_repair.py` | Dedicated conversation repair node: gives a read-only RepairPlanner exact diff paths plus verified frontend diagnostic source paths, executes bounded shared SmallTask tasks with the current plan strategy, persists the plan, and returns successful repairs to `validate_direct_fix`. Missing or placeholder paths fail with evidence; explicit formal revision escalation keeps its confirmation gate. | Read with `graph/direct_modification_workflow.py`, `services/direct_validation.py`, `services/small_task.py`, `agents/repair_planner/`, and `protocols/direct_modification_projection.py`. |
| `Backend/app/graph/state.py`                            | `ProjectState` TypedDict for compact Graph State: project/request/workspace metadata, selected targets, EntitySourceBinding submission, formal artifact paths, and requirement-revision transaction identity. Application configuration prerequisites are held by the direct conversation checkpoint and its request-bound operation; RequirementSpec keeps only business authorization clarification. It also holds checkpoint-internal TechnicalPlan repair candidate/errors, runtime `project_plan` context, Build DAG confirmation/progress, explicit `resume_execution_run_id` for DAG Recovery source selection, `test_phase_confirmation`, stable `test_target`, build/test summaries plus separate Markdown/internal-JSON test-report paths, unit-test generation context/mapping/change sets, quality gate, SmallTask state, code-review scan/repair/build state, `acceptance_phase_confirmation`, launch/acceptance evidence, failures, and timeline. Internal TechnicalPlan repair fields are excluded from AG-UI projection and never become formal artifacts; `project_plan` is a runtime projection for the current Build scope. Workspace `build-task-plan.json` remains the DAG confirmation authority. Build-gate/DAG-generation retry returns to `prepare_build_tasks`; ordinary task retry resumes `build`; completed Build pauses at `test_phase_confirmation` before `integration_test`, and clean review pauses at `acceptance_phase_confirmation` before the acceptance subgraph. | Read when changing workflow state keys, persisted file references, readiness/binding resume contracts, DAG/test/acceptance confirmation, code-review repair recovery, stale-checkpoint recovery, node contracts, or planning artifacts. |
| `Backend/app/graph/nodes/`                              | Thin phase nodes for classification, requirements, planning/detail confirmation, task preparation, direct modification, build/testing wrappers, and lifecycle phases. Task preparation checks confirmed formal artifacts, template readiness, scope detail, and API consistency before compiling a seven-stage DAG-generation snapshot through the shared progress service. Platform-owned DAG validation failures route to bounded model regeneration or `handle_failure`; they are not exposed as user task-splitting decisions. | Read the file matching the phase before changing node behavior; keep deterministic routing/state updates here and delegate reusable rules to `services/`.                                                           |
| `Backend/app/graph/nodes/tasks.py` | Shared formal-artifact, Endpoint API Design readiness, and scope projections for the current async Unit planner. Production task generation and confirmation are owned by `task_planning_adapter.py`; the removed Scope-level generator and replacement merge are not restored. | Read with `Backend/app/graph/nodes/task_planning_adapter.py` before changing planning inputs or readiness gates. |
| `Backend/app/graph/subgraphs/build.py`                  | Build Subgraph behind `build`: at Build Run start it reads the current confirmed workspace `build-task-plan.json`, requires `build-dag.v4`, `status=ready`, a matching frozen `template_context`, `task_graph.validation.is_valid=true`, and matching Build scope, then binds a read-only per-run copy by canonical SHA-256. Before every leaf dispatch it checks that the planning authority still has that digest; drift terminates the Run rather than switching plans. After all Build tasks succeed, it replays the confirmed DAG's platform Route/authorization/AuthConstants projections, records separate `platform_projection_evidence`, and only then performs read-only EDD. It dispatches each ready task through an independent bounded Agent invocation, combines the task's authorized snapshot paths with that invocation's recorded write/delete tool paths, and checks scope only against the resulting task-owned change set so concurrent tasks cannot inherit each other's violations. Engineering acceptance always runs; business acceptance runs independently only when `DEVAGENTSTUDIO_DAG_BUSINESS_SELF_CHECK_ENABLED=true`, otherwise it records an explicit skipped status before the aggregated Repair decision. Database tasks continue to use database execution evidence. FAILED re-entry preserves completed tasks and retries local failures or prepared repairs; INTERRUPTED/explicit Build restart creates a fresh Build Run. Both refresh workspace facts; repair-scope/database-approval continuations remain bounded. | Read when changing task dispatch, concurrency, task-owned file attribution, Build Run plan binding, platform projection timing, acceptance aggregation, confirmation recovery, owner result collection, explicit retry recovery, scheduler coordination after code generation, database approval pauses, or repair planning. |
| `Backend/app/domain/api_design.py` | Current-only Pydantic contract for confirmed `endpoint-field-mapping.v7` documents. `api_design_fields.py`, `api_design_values.py`, and `api_design_database.py` split shared fields, value rules, and single-table query/write contracts. `sourceBinding` preserves the selected object; `externalApiBindings` owns upstream request targets; `value_mapping` owns business/fixed/context outputs. See `docs/FIELD_VALUE_RULES.md`. | Read before changing Endpoint field mappings, database writes, query trees, CRUD rules, or source snapshots. |
| `Backend/app/services/api_design.py`                    | Deterministic Endpoint API-design service: creates immutable Endpoint field snapshots, enumerates real-time source candidates, validates CRUD partitioning, write-value and query types, complete `fieldMappings`/`databaseWrites`, redacted source snapshots, and current design readiness. TechnicalPlan global entity semantics remain outside Endpoint↔Source mappings. | Read with the domain model, data-source catalog service, Endpoint document store, and node before changing design behavior. |
| `Backend/app/services/api_design_mapping_rules.py` | Current v7 mapping rules for response-read/external source identities and processing descriptions; database write targets are validated through `databaseWrites`, while query conditions use the database query tree. | Read with the domain and API design service when changing mapping cardinality or source identity. |
| `Backend/app/workspace/endpoint_design_documents.py`   | Reads and atomically writes `.devagentstudio/plans/endpoints/endpoint--<contractId>--<endpointId>.json/.md`, computes the TechnicalPlan contract fingerprint, and requires the confirmed dual-file pair for readiness. | Read before changing Endpoint artifact naming, fingerprinting, Markdown rendering, or persistence guarantees. |
| `Backend/app/graph/nodes/api_design.py`                 | Implements deterministic `api_design_readiness_gate`; independent mapping save writes Endpoint artifacts before this gate. Page readiness checks every `requiredEndpointId`, Endpoint readiness checks only the selected Contract/Endpoint, and confirmation rechecks the complete displayed revision set before entering `inspect_workspace`. | Read with workflow request/lifecycle/projection/runtime adapters and the frontend API-design panel. |
| `Backend/app/graph/subgraphs/testing.py`                | Testing Subgraph behind `integration_test`: runs dependency installation, frontend/backend Build checks, pauses through an AG-UI confirmation for the optional advisory frontend performance test, launches the user `frontend` dev server with `frontend_performance_runner`, records the non-blocking Lighthouse check, and performs the integration quality gate. Every quality-gate pass overwrites the internal JSON and user-facing Markdown test reports so repair retries always publish the latest result; it no longer calls TestGenerationAgent or executes frontend/backend unit tests. Any blocking integration check routes to the independent `small_task_repair` loop; API contracts are validated before build and repair plans never route directly to `build`. | Read when changing integration build/performance confirmation, quality gate rules, test evidence shape (including `performanceScores`/`performanceMetrics`), test-report refresh, revision requests, integration repair budgets, repair scope, or repair-task routing. |
| `Backend/app/graph/subgraphs/code_review.py`             | Code Review Subgraph behind the top-level `code_review` node: runs one read-only scan, streams only the current authorized source-file relative path through the ephemeral `code_review.scan`/`codeReviewScan` AG-UI projection, persists the immutable latest Markdown snapshot through `workspace/code_review_documents.py`, pauses on `code_review_repair_confirmation`, performs bounded Skill-driven repair, requires real dedicated pnpm-install evidence for declared dependency actions, and runs independent frontend/backend build checks with a three-round repair loop. Scan/repair model failures publish the bounded `codeReviewRetry` target consumed by `workflowAction=retry_code_review`; repair retries reuse the issue snapshot and failed request's iteration, while build/budget failures remain non-retryable through this action. It preserves the original scan result/report, captures authorized project and generated lockfile Diff while excluding node_modules, reuses successful Agent install evidence, and emits bounded scan/repair/build projections on one stable process-step identity before `acceptance_phase_confirmation`. | Read when changing review reports, live reviewed-file display, one-click review repair, model-request retry recovery, frontend project scope, dedicated package-install evidence, independent review build logs, repair iteration limits, or review AG-UI progress. |
| `Backend/app/graph/subgraphs/acceptance.py`              | Acceptance Subgraph mounted directly as the top-level `acceptance` runnable: reuses the deterministic project launcher as `launch_project`, streams its custom progress through the parent AG-UI run, skips launch when a successful preview snapshot is already present, and enters `acceptance_review` to project `page_acceptance` with preview/launch/request evidence. Launch failures end the subgraph without an acceptance interaction; accepted/finalize behavior remains available at the main graph boundary. | Read when changing acceptance-phase launch, preview recovery, `page_acceptance` lifecycle mapping, or acceptance AG-UI state projection. |
| `Backend/app/graph/subgraphs/unit_testing.py`            | Development Unit Testing Subgraph behind `unit_test`: captures the first Build `code_changes/code_change_sets`, derives the bounded `unit_test_generation_context.code_diff`, and gives TestGenerationAgent only changed sources/existing tests, the confirmed Build DAG, current TechnicalPlan JSON, and the active Build scope/slice. It handles the existing run/skip confirmation, validates generated files, executes affected frontend/backend unit tests, writes an independent unit report/quality gate, and plans the bounded `unit_test_repair` loop. | `Backend/app/services/repository_branch.py`, `Backend/app/services/git_branch.py`, `Backend/app/services/git_credentials.py`, `Backend/app/services/git_identity.py` | Own the application's remote-repository branch. `git_branch.py` holds the Git ref-name rules (shared by branch actions and commit-push; it depends on nothing, because both callers importing each other would be a cycle) and `RepositoryBranchError`'s successor `GitBranchError`. `repository_branch.py` owns `git ls-remote` existence checks, `create_local_branch` (checkout -b + push, used when an iteration starts on a new branch), `delete_remote_branch`, and `push_baseline_to_branch`. **The workspace's local branch must always match the application branch name**: historical preview (`git rev-parse <branch>`) and read-only source (`git show <branch>:<path>`) both resolve the branch locally, so `push_baseline_to_branch` calls `_ensure_local_branch` first — `git push HEAD:refs/heads/<branch>` only creates the branch on the *remote*, leaving the local repo on the `git init` default and making the application's first branch permanently unviewable; every action returns a `{branchName, status, commitSha, message}` result and **never raises**. `read_workspace_repository_target` reads `repoUrl`/`branchName`/`branchOverwriteConfirmed` from the workspace `application.json`; an empty branch name means the application has no configured branch and every caller skips. Only a user-confirmed overwrite force-pushes, so a divergent remote branch is refused rather than silently destroyed. `git_credentials.py` owns the credential source for every remote push: `DEVAGENTSTUDIO_GIT_CREDENTIAL_SOURCE=env` (default, outside the company) injects `DEVAGENTSTUDIO_GIT_USERNAME`/`DEVAGENTSTUDIO_GIT_TOKEN` into the URL, while `helper` (inside the company) passes the bare URL so the local Git credential helper (Windows Credential Manager / macOS Keychain) authenticates and the server validates the employee's own repository permission. It also supplies `git_env()` (`GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT=0`, because the Electron-launched backend has no TTY) and classifies authentication failures into mode-specific, actionable messages. `git_identity.py` owns the commit author identity: the company Gitee validates the author email (`git002 Unauthorized email account cannot submit code`), so the baseline commit uses `DEVAGENTSTUDIO_GIT_AUTHOR_NAME`/`_EMAIL` when both are set, else the machine's global `git config user.email`, else a placeholder that at least lets Bootstrap finish. The template baseline commit message is fixed to the company board-number format in `workspace_bootstrap/git_manager.py` (`_TEMPLATE_BASELINE_COMMIT_MESSAGE`). | Read with `protocols/repository_branch.py`, `services/version_publish.py` (`build_authenticated_remote_url` is the single credential-injection point and defers to `services/git_credentials.py`), `services/revision_preview.py`, and `services/workspace_bootstrap/service.py` before changing branch rules, remote push behavior, the credential source, or the commit identity. |

| `Backend/app/services/build_task_planner.py`, `engineering_acceptance.py`, and `business_acceptance.py` | Compile workspace-aware model candidates into `build-dag.v4`, including structural task normalization, cross-platform file scopes, dependencies, owner semantics, execution batches, deterministic engineering `acceptance_checks`, deliverables, and formal-source-bound business checks. Structural normalization covers field aliases, path/operation defaults, `target_files` plus `allowed_paths` fallback, exact duplicate-task merging, Unit compilation, and fingerprints; it must not rewrite semantic task boundaries. After business-check compilation, `build_task_planner.py` enforces one normal frontend implementation owner per stable `api_contract_id + endpoint_id`; a permission-bound endpoint requires a `backend.endpoint_controller` deliverable with the exact Endpoint target when the module belongs to the current Build scope. Permissions never create a separate Unit. Conflicts stay in `task_graph.validation.errors`, while repair tasks inherit their parent ownership rather than creating a second owner. Model-returned acceptance text and verification commands are discarded. Engineering checks cover file/scope, frontend page structure, platform-sliced page Action permissions, and platform-sliced Endpoint permissions: a target Controller handler located by exact HTTP method/path must carry exactly one `RequireAnyResource` containing precisely the supplied `AuthConstants` ANY-OF symbols. `business_acceptance.py` compiles the nine deterministic business kinds with stable formal-artefact hashes, aggregates sibling deliverables of the same kind into one bounded target-path set, and allows same-Contract frontend API deliverables or historical incremental Tasks to share their canonical module path while separating `required_endpoints` from retained `allowed_existing_endpoints`; composite Entity/PO/DTO/Converter or Repository/Mapper implementations are verified together rather than per file. Deliverable ownership remains keyed by formal responsibility, not physical path; path, Unit, source, and verifier errors remain in `task_graph.validation.errors` for bounded regeneration. `reconcile_live_page_paths` only maps a non-existing candidate page path to one already-existing unique semantic directory without changing its declared operation; Unit metadata and cross-Unit dependency compilation live in `build_unit_compiler.py`, which slices each backend module task to the Endpoint IDs named by its deliverables. | Read when changing task ids, structural normalization, file scopes, owners, endpoint implementation ownership, deliverables, or engineering/business check compilation, DAG validation, or automatic regeneration. Read `engineering_acceptance_verifier.py` and `business_acceptance_verifier.py` with scheduler changes, `build_task_menu.py` for read-only page-path reconciliation, and `build_unit_compiler.py` for Unit dependencies and fingerprints. |

The required immutable mapping is saved with the lightweight PlanningRun projection; live events, final results, and exact Run/Thread-matched lifecycle/recovery reads all use it without rereading TechnicalPlan.
