The operation fact binds the exact old DraftIdentity, thread, current execution Run, consumption phase, and generated PlanningRun so Native Recovery can resume after the old Pending is consumed without treating an absent Pending as authorization. Scope Assembly computes stable `review_task_ids`, `platform_task_ids`, `new_task_ids`, and `reused_task_ids`; Pending persistence validates `planning_provenance.schema_version=planning-provenance.v2` before canonical SHA-256 signing. Recovery stores only the final failed source Run's current ready Candidate bodies after `UNIT_GENERATION_INFRASTRUCTURE_FAILURE`; it is not PlanningRun/Pending/Formal authority. The exact Native Recovery source may supply Candidates to either ordinary generation or an already-consumed Regenerate attempt; both recheck current inputs and fail open to full generation if Snapshot is unavailable. Recovered Candidates receive a new identity and source provenance without a model Attempt. The Pending writer binds backend metadata, rejects caller identity/failed validation, and writes atomically without touching Formal. Confirm checks exact request identity, self digest, current Formal baseline, fresh inputs, Scope and DAG gate, then atomically replaces Formal after removing both `draft_identity` and `planning_provenance`, before deleting matching Pending. Route Projection is not a DAG task or persisted route baseline: after a successful (including empty application-scope) DAG, `graph/subgraphs/build.py` invokes the v2 template contract and records only captured Workspace changes in Build evidence. | Read `docs/BUILD_TASK_PLAN_CONFIRM_PROMOTION.md`, `docs/DAG_TASK_GENERATION_UNIT_CANDIDATE_PLAN.md`, `docs/SEQUENTIAL_DAG_PLANNING.md`, `docs/template/route-projection-post-dag-finalization-plan.md`, `Backend/tests/test_planning_recovery.py`, `test_build_task_plan_lifecycle.py`, `test_build_task_plan_regenerate.py`, `test_dag_confirm_authority_cutover.py`, and `test_pending_build_task_plan_documents.py` before changing promotion, identity, persistence, Recovery, Regenerate, application-level exclusivity, Route Projection finalization, or refresh/cancellation behavior. |
| `Backend/app/workspace/workspace_snapshot_documents.py` | Helpers for reading workspace snapshot JSON and resolving the `.devagentstudio/cache` artifact root used by `inspect_workspace`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | Read when changing workspace snapshot persistence paths or cache loading.                                                                                                                                           |
| `Backend/app/workspace/test_documents.py`               | Atomically writes internal quality-gate data to `.devagentstudio/reports/test-report.json` and renders the current user-facing `.devagentstudio/reports/test-report.md`. The Markdown always summarizes frontend/backend Build checks, conditionally includes bounded Lighthouse scores/metrics, and strips full logs, HTML bodies, and host absolute paths. | Read with Testing Subgraph and workflow projection when changing test report persistence, rendering, sanitization, or artifact paths. |
| `Backend/app/workspace/code_review_documents.py`        | Renders and atomically overwrites `.devagentstudio/reports/code-review.md` from the normalized scan snapshot. Diff reports include the complete server-validated reviewed-file list; full reports publish only front/back scan roots and counts. Repair actions, logs, and host absolute paths are excluded. | Read with the Code Review Subgraph and workflow projection when changing the review report contract or persistence path. |
| `Backend/app/middleware/approvals.py`                   | Approval grants, reusable operation rules, and protected-operation fingerprints.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Read when changing approval lifecycle, scopes, or risk gating.                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Backend/app/persistence/run_store.py`                  | Writes `.devagentstudio/runs/<runId>` artifacts and JSONL run events.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Read when changing run artifact lifecycle, formats, or retention.                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Backend/app/persistence/checkpoints.py`                | Owns workspace-scoped LangGraph SQLite checkpointers for the main workflow, including `.devagentstudio/checkpoints/checkpoints.sqlite` path creation, optional global `DEVAGENTSTUDIO_CHECKPOINT_DB` override, conservative retention cleanup, exact session-thread row deletion, target-workspace row deletion, and connection shutdown. Session deletion checks recorded workspace identity before deleting a thread from a shared database. Full-app deletion identifies rows from checkpoint channel values, metadata, and known lifecycle threads; it removes only matching rows from a shared override DB, while a local DB connection is closed before its project directory is trashed. | Read when changing workflow state persistence, checkpoint retention, recovery/deletion behavior, or `DEVAGENTSTUDIO_CHECKPOINT_DB` handling. |
| `Backend/app/observability/agent_events.py`             | Helpers for append-oriented agent run event payloads.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | Read when changing event names or JSONL fields.                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `Backend/app/observability/telemetry_models.py`, `telemetry.py` | Phase-one optional-field telemetry models and backend-only emit entry points. The caller captures occurrence time and submits validation, missing-field defaults, JSON assembly, and one-record POST to a bounded two-thread executor. Producer defaults include only OS and product version from `Frontend/package.json` (included by `Backend/packaging/devagentstudio-backend.spec`); source/environment remain caller-supplied. The temporary default user is `Hupy/test`; explicit values/nulls are preserved. There is no return value, persistent queue, or retry. The URL is read from `DEVAGENTSTUDIO_TELEMETRY_URL` by `telemetry.py::telemetry_post_url`. | Read with `docs/design/observability/telemetry-phase1-v1.jsonc` and `telemetry-usage.md` when changing field shape, business call sites, executor limits, POST behavior, or runtime configuration. |
| `Backend/app/utils/model_output.py`                     | Shared best-effort JSON object extraction for direct model outputs, including fenced or commentary-wrapped JSON.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Read when changing model JSON parsing used by requirements, product/technical planning, task preparation, or entity-source binding.                                                                                |

## Current Build DAG Contract

Build 运行时采用逐任务补位：`services/build_dispatch.py` 持有整轮最多三个工作线程，复制派发时的状态与运行上下文，用 `FIRST_COMPLETED` 收集单任务结果；`graph/subgraphs/build.py` 每次提交验收结果、文件变更及执行记录后重新选择就绪任务。`services/build_scheduler.py` 同时排除所有 running 任务的写锁。执行记录沿用 `batch_committed` 阶段，一次提交一个任务，恢复时将尚无提交结果的 running 任务重新置为 pending。需要确认或取消时停止补位并收集在途结果；修复规划在独立任务和在途任务收敛后运行。数据库任务按单任务派发以匹配现有单审批载荷。回归入口为 `tests.test_build_rolling_dispatch`，覆盖依赖即时补位、并发上限、持续写锁、独立失败、确认收尾和中间执行记录恢复。

The current Build path is `confirmed formal artifacts + template readiness + PageImplementationContract + confirmed Endpoint API designs → api_design_readiness_gate → inspect_workspace → prepare_build_tasks → DAG confirmation → build`. The following boundaries are part of the current contract and should be checked together when changing this path:

| Concern | Current contract | Read first |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Runtime context | `project_plan` is a read-only runtime projection used to assemble the current Build context. It is not a new editable formal artifact, and DAG generation/confirmation must not write it back into TechnicalPlan JSON. | `Backend/app/graph/nodes/tasks.py`, `Backend/app/services/build_context_resolver.py`, `Backend/app/workspace/plan_documents.py` |
| DAG prerequisites | Confirmed `RequirementSpec`, `ProductPlan`, `TechnicalPlan`, frozen `TemplateState`/`template_context`, Endpoint semantics and confirmed Endpoint API designs must pass before candidate generation. Page development additionally requires the confirmed `UiManifest` and current PageImplementationContract; individual pages may have no generated UI reference. Independent Endpoint development does not consume UI artifacts. EntitySourceBinding is not a normal Build prerequisite. | `Backend/app/graph/nodes/tasks.py`, `Backend/app/services/stage_prerequisites.py`, `Backend/app/services/template_state.py`, `Backend/app/services/build_context_resolver.py` |
| Generation and validation | The platform owns Unit/task boundaries. Candidate structural normalization may fill deterministic fields and merge exact duplicates; compiled business checks also enforce one frontend implementation owner per stable Endpoint identity. It must not delete or rewrite semantic menu/route/page-boundary tasks. Violations stay in `task_graph.validation.errors`; `task_preparer.py` feeds them back for bounded automatic regeneration, and exhausted attempts become platform failure rather than a user task-splitting request. | `Backend/app/services/build_task_planner.py`, `Backend/app/agents/main/task_preparer_prompt.py`, `Backend/app/agents/main/task_preparer.py`, `Backend/app/services/build_task_menu.py` |
| Deliverable contract | `deliverables[].kind` must use the exact `DELIVERABLE_KINDS` allowlist; values outside it are rejected and do not receive a business acceptance projection. Existing `deliverables[].paths` behavior remains unchanged. | `Backend/app/agents/main/task_preparer_prompt.py`, `Backend/app/services/business_acceptance.py` |
| Artifact authority | Formal `build-task-plan.json` and Pending `build-task-plan.pending.json` are separate. Exact Pending confirmation promotes Formal. Build binds an immutable per-run copy using a planning-content SHA-256. Before dispatch, `.devagentstudio/plans/build-runs/execution-<SHA-256 of Workflow run ID>.json` binds Workflow run, thread, scope, Build Run and formal digest; each committed batch atomically updates its internal tasks/results there. A recovery child records its own execution identity and the validated inherited baseline in `progress`, with `progress_source_run_id` naming the original progress owner. `execution_stage` distinguishes `bound`, `inherited`, `prepared`, and `batch_committed`; a selected retry or ready repair persists its prepared tasks, retry counts and old results before Runner dispatch, and later batches replace this with child-owned committed progress. Recovery checks the immutable plan and task contracts and follows only the verified RecoveryAttempt parent chain to matching Build progress. For a child interrupted before preparing its retry, the exact started FAILED-origin Build RecoveryAttempt and its fork parent authorize re-preparation. Ordinary interrupted continuation restores progress without activating failed-task retry; a prepared retry continues its pending work without incrementing the retry count again. Scheduler status/evidence writeback remains hash-neutral and atomically updates matching Formal tasks under the promotion lock. Pending identity is unchanged. | `Backend/app/workspace/task_documents.py`, `Backend/app/services/build_task_plan_lifecycle.py`, `Backend/app/graph/subgraphs/build.py`, `Backend/app/services/execution_recovery_executor.py` |
| Ownership boundary | Agents own real page implementation; after all tasks succeed, the platform replays the confirmed DAG to write shared routes, optional authorization resources, and AuthConstants. Bootstrap and planning do not create page placeholders, menus, routes, or branch-dependent artifacts. For real APIs, the Frontend Agent generates business API modules that reuse the template-owned `service.ts`; no generated response-adapter Task or file is part of the Build DAG. Static modules do not use HTTP transport. Normal Build does not create `database:*` Units or database-owner tasks. | `Backend/app/agents/main/task_preparer_prompt.py`, `Backend/app/agents/frontend/generator.py`, `Backend/app/graph/nodes/tasks.py`, `Backend/app/graph/subgraphs/build.py` |
| User confirmation | The AG-UI `build_task_plan_confirmation` mode is read-only and exposes the identity-bound `confirm`、`abandon` and `regenerate` actions. It shows the current target, directly related Endpoints, one topologically ordered `reviewTasks` list tagged `new`/`reused`, and a status-only summary for unrelated retained tasks. Confirm resumes the original checkpoint; abandon marks the pending plan abandoned, stops the execution, and releases its locks. Frontend ownership is split: left `MessageList`/`ProcessSteps/DagGenerationProgress` keeps Unit progress and the completion navigation; right `RightPanelTabs` shows `待确认任务` only for the current PendingPlan owner and `StageOutputPanel` contains the only full confirmation form. First navigation is keyed by DraftIdentity and runs once per app/owner; explicit left navigation remains repeatable, and consumed Pending falls back from the right tab without reopening a closed panel. Platform validation/generation failures are not manually corrected. | `Backend/app/protocols/workflow/request.py`, `Backend/app/protocols/workflow/run_control.py`, `Backend/app/protocols/workflow/definition.py`, `Frontend/src/renderer/src/components/AiChatPanel/AiChatPanel.tsx`, `Frontend/src/renderer/src/components/AiChatPanel/dagPendingPlanPresentation.ts`, `Frontend/src/renderer/src/components/AiChatPanel/components/MessageList/`, `Frontend/src/renderer/src/components/AiChatPanel/components/ProcessSteps/DagGenerationProgress.tsx`, `Frontend/src/renderer/src/components/AiChatPanel/components/StageOutputPanel/`, `Frontend/tests/dagGenerationProgress.test.ts`, `docs/DAG_TASK_GENERATION_AND_VALIDATION_MININAL_CHANGE_PLAN.md` |

### Merge integration: task planning and Build execution

- 后端模块提示词按通用路径、foundation、business、controller 分组；既有实现沿用原路径增量修改，新增职责使用规范目录，不隐式迁移。每个接口仍独立成任务，但默认在模块共享 ApplicationService、Assembler 中追加方法；Converter 按来源/目标模型复用，数据库与外部 API 保持访问和传输边界。首个负责的业务任务新增共享文件，后续任务修改同一路径并保留已有成员，不把业务实现挪入基础任务。DTO 仅在字段、校验与语义一致时复用。文件说明按实际职责选择栏目并逐项换行，不强制五栏：数据对象写字段及必要类型约束，接口写签名及必要调用语义，PO/Feign 补充映射或绑定；Service、RepositoryImpl、适配器和转换器仅描述各自承担的逻辑与边界，直接映射简述，不逐文件重复业务算法或通用规范。`backend_module_candidate.py` 保留多行字符串，确认卡既有 `white-space: pre-wrap` 原样展示，任务级说明仍沿用确定性拼接去重。`covers` 只标注实际职责，并给出共享 PO 覆盖多个端点的条件示例。目录树不能证明已有方法签名或实现兼容性，契约目录缺少端点标签时不推测关联。
- Backend module planning groups the scoped responsibilities into one foundation task when needed, one business task per Endpoint, and a shared Controller task. `backend_unit_task_rules.py` describes the file plan, `backend_module_candidate.py` binds it to deterministic tasks and deliverables, and `backend_module_scope.py` derives stage dependencies and filters execution to the selected endpoints. The shared Controller retains per-Endpoint authorization and completion evidence. `field_value_prompt_rules.py` supplies the planning and implementation rules for typed values, source bindings, and missing values. Configuration examples live in `docs/FIELD_MAPPING_EXAMPLES.md`.
- 后端任务规划的 `backend_unit_task_rules.py` 通过已有内置资源解析器只读读取 `springboot-template-modification-boundary/references/module-layout.md` 的编号文件表，合并同目录职责为简短路径表，按数据源筛选持久层并补充外部 Client 目录，不整篇内联示例工程树。Skill 和执行 Agent 提示词不改动。新增文件按 `domain`、`infrastructure`、`application`、`adapter/web` 规划；基础任务准备空仓储接口与实现骨架，单接口业务任务补齐方法和必要转换，不隐式搬迁已有文件。规划检查项按实际来源与阶段选择，数据库 Unit 不注入 static/external API 详细规则；共享 `field_value_prompt_rules.py` 继续供既有其他规划及执行流程使用。
- `unit_task_prompt.py::_prompt_context` 对所有 backend Unit（含 Bootstrap）的提示词投影仅保留 `workspace_context.backend.dir_structure`，完整保留该目录树原文，排除前端/全局目录、代码索引、扫描统计及其他工作区元数据。冻结 Context、输入指纹和校验仍使用完整原始快照，其他类型 Unit 的提示词上下文不受影响。
- `unit_task_prompt.py` 将 backend Unit 路由到 `backend_unit_task_prompt.py`：提示词按目标、单份职责表、冻结契约目录与后端目录树、规划规则、原输出格式五部分构造，仅保留必要 JSON，不投影 PlanningRun ID、指纹、基线摘要或完整 Context。模块表直接复用 `backend_stage_manifest` 的 key 和 covers 序号；Bootstrap 使用精确 requirement/kind/target/provides 表，不叠加通用职责 JSON。非空 retained 摘要及 Global/最新 Local 诊断才追加，工具 ref_id/selector/nextCursor 边界、Candidate 输出协议、校验及确认门保持不变，不影响前端 Unit 提示词。
- `Backend/app/workspace/task_documents.py` retains scheduler runtime writeback to the matching Formal DAG and stores a per-Workflow-execution Build binding/progress record before dispatch and after each committed batch. Runtime status, evidence and timestamps do not change the Build planning digest; Pending draft identity and exact confirmation checks remain separate. Build Run plan snapshots remain immutable.
- Bootstrap source changes use exact `backend.bootstrap:database` / `backend.bootstrap:external_api` requirements in the new Unit planner. Existing tasks cannot hide a missing source capability. Regression: `Backend/tests/test_bootstrap_scope_replanning.py`.

## Frontend

构建执行隐藏范围标识、进度条及完成百分比，保留执行进度标题与完成数量；五项统计和轮次卡片由 `components/AiChatPanel/components/WorkflowRunCard/index.tsx` 保留；下方任务交给 `BuildExecutionTaskList.tsx` 与同名 LESS，按正式 Unit/owner 分为前端、后端及确有其他归属时的其他任务，组内沿用执行状态/时间排序。原生按钮标头展示任务图标、标题及真实执行结果；成功与失败任务仅在标题栏使用绿色/红色背景、图标及执行结果文字区分状态；卡片正文与内部折叠行保持中性底色，内部图标、计数和操作提示保留紫色。`BuildExecutionTaskAppearance.less` 单独负责状态外观与窄屏换行，成功任务不重复显示底部完成标签；复用、检查点和原有资格控制的重试留在详情底部，支持明暗主题。`BuildExecutionTaskDetails.tsx` 复用确认卡的 `TaskDisclosureHeader.tsx`、`BuildTaskFileList.tsx`、文件投影及折叠样式，分别折叠接口（名称/彩色方法/路径）、依赖名称、文件改动、范围、工程/业务检查及执行信息；文件名下显示路径，展开后保留多行说明，任务级确定性重复说明省略。失败原因和真实验收证据保留，任务/文件收起不丢展开状态，运行中的工具活动在标题下独立区域或详情中唯一展示。`buildExecutionPresentation.ts` 与 `buildExecutionAcceptance.ts` 分别整理执行展示和验收证据。后端 `services/build_execution_projection.py` 在 `build_scheduler.resolve_execution_slice` 的返回副本中补充 `backendStage`、`coveredEndpoints`、完整任务集合解析的 `dependencyTitles`，通过现有 AG-UI 到前端类型；不回写原任务、不改变调度、统计、状态、重试或正式确认门。

计划确认使用淡紫色折叠分区，任务执行使用中性底色的轻量列表行；共用图标底座和紧跟标题的数量徽标，展开时强调底色及细边框。`TaskDisclosureHeader.tsx` 按去重后的明确文件统计各操作数量，未知或缺失操作不推测，目录范围不计数。`TaskFileOperationBadge.tsx` 与同名 LESS 提供新增绿色、修改/重命名橘黄色、删除警示色的文字徽标，避开通用 `.ant-tag` 灰色覆盖。`BuildTaskFileList` 使用独立文件卡片、88px 最小行高与路径上方留白；收起只展示操作、文件名及完整路径，展开保留原始多行说明，使用随明暗主题适配的淡灰详情底色，未提供说明保留提示。窄面板允许名称、路径与操作摘要换行并为箭头留出空间；各级折叠沿用 Ant Design 的键盘行为与内部状态保留。 `TaskFileDescription.tsx` 与同名 LESS 只按原文已有的行首栏目分成标签/正文详情行，“字段”“类定义”与其他栏目使用相同正文样式，不另加代码框；不补造必填栏目、不改写约束，无法识别的说明完整保留。所有文件直接列出，不合并为“其他文件”。 无前置依赖时直接显示“无”；执行信息暂不展示实现说明同步摘要或路径，并按实际可见项目计数。业务检查隐藏“DAG 业务自检已通过环境变量关闭，本次自动跳过。”配置提示，保留原始数据、检查状态与其他证据。执行失败摘要位于“执行信息”折叠行，展开后保留完整失败原因、分类及工程/业务证据；不编造错误数量或扩大重试资格。

Workflow 事件内容由 `Backend/app/protocols/workflow/event_projection.py` 统一裁剪，正式 Runtime 与 `workflow_visualization.py` 共用。`projection.py::_workflow_event_state` 仅选取阶段、状态、目标身份、Markdown 路径、有限 timeline 及既有有界 workspaceInspection，`stateDelta` 只记录同 Run 事件序列中真正变化的展示字段（显式 null 仍表示清空）；它不是 Graph State 回放或执行恢复协议。事件保留 sequence、node、attempt、status、message 和错误诊断，detail 中整份计划改为文档摘要，原始 buildEvents/buildResults/testEvents 仅记数量，Build Slice 任务不重复带 source_refs 或内部 acceptance_checks，展示验收项与失败原因保留。完整 state/result、正式计划及 checkpoint 合同维持当前用途；部分继续动作仍消费 resumeState，不能单独删去这些快照。`/health` 的 workflow.eventStorage 发布当前事件内容边界。Frontend `src/shared/workflowEventProjection.ts` 在 `service/agUiAgent.ts` 接收和 `main/sessionMessageNormalization.ts` 深拷贝/落盘前应用同样约束，按 Run 隔离增量且不删除失败轮次。当前没有新增 Run 仓库、内容寻址工件存储、失败 trace 清理、gzip 格式或历史迁移；未加载的本地历史原文仍按原范围保留。回归：`tests.test_workflow_event_projection`（包含真实受控 AG-UI 流及重复大状态体积）、`tests.test_workflow_ag_ui`、`pnpm test:session-history` 中的 `workflowEventProjection.test.ts`，以及聊天步骤、权限、连接恢复相关前端测试。

本地聊天历史由 `Frontend/src/main/sessionHistory.ts`、`sessionFileReader.ts` 和 `sessionFileWriter.ts` 负责，唯一正文源仍是 `<sessionId>.json`，元数据在前、messages 在末尾。列表只读最多 256 KiB 文件头，不读取或生成 `.json.index`；非空且未计数的 `messageCount` 明确为 null，空会话为 0，阶段恢复和会话选择不能把未知数当成空会话。`sessions:read` 保持 before 消息 ID 游标，默认及最大每页 10 条，从文件尾以 64 KiB 块反向定位完整 JSON 对象；UTF-8 仅在完整边界解码，正确处理跨块转义和嵌套，不依赖消息 ID 单调性。Worker 最多缓存 8 个文件各 256 个访问位置，按文件身份/大小/时间失效，不构建全文索引；读取过程中磁盘版本变化明确失败。单条消息不设置大小拒绝或占位阈值，选中的消息完整读取并进入 loadedMessageIds。整页 4 MiB 仅作为减少分页条数的软预算，首条消息超过预算也完整返回，游标可继续向前；它不是单条消息或整个应用的内存上限。历史只返回当前窗口，不额外扫描/回填较早控制消息，当前运行、确认和恢复权限仍沿原 Backend AG-UI Lifecycle/checkpoint 获取；未遍历全文的 history.total 为 null。

`sessions:save.loadedMessageIds` 只替换/移除已完整加载范围，`sessionFileWriter.ts` 用固定块扫描及复制未加载原文，每条新消息独立序列化，处理短写，先写同目录临时文件再原子发布，外部并发改写时拒绝覆盖；保存仍需顺序读取/复制原文件，但不会把原文件整体读入内存或重新建立索引。`sessionStorageWorker.ts` 保持单 Worker 串行读写及故障隔离。`useSessionHistory.ts` 保留会话切换、失败重试及迟到页隔离；`sessionMessageCache.ts` 按已返回页顺序前插，不混合保存前后字节偏移排序，不复活本地已移除消息。`useWorkflowConversation.ts` 最终保存仍读取同步更新后的当前缓存，确认门、Thread、checkpoint 和草稿不由展示分页拥有。`MessageList/HistoryMessageRow.tsx` 和 `useHistoryScroll.ts` 保持视口挂载与阅读锚点。回归：`pnpm test:session-history`（包含无索引 210 MiB 文件的实际读量、巨型消息保存哈希、字节预算与跨块转义）、`pnpm test:performance-retention`、`pnpm test:connection-recovery`、`pnpm test:stage-sessions`；UI 必须在 Electron 验证。

设计稿展示读取由 `hooks/uiDesignPageSource.ts` 按工作区共享，`useUiDesignPagesWithCode.ts` 只在初次进入、页面状态变化、重新显示/聚焦时校准，queued/generating 期间串行轮询，终态停止；相同源码/状态沿用对象引用。`hooks/uiReadVisibility.ts` 仅暂停隐藏工作台的展示读取，不控制后台 AG-UI、生成池或执行恢复。会话正文由 `AiChatPanel/hooks/sessionMessageCache.ts` 管理，最多缓存 3 个无保护的已保存历史且估算体积合计不超过 32MiB；当前/预加载交接、执行中、保存中/失败/未保存、待确认及暂停恢复记录受保护。`useChatSessions.ts` 在保存成功边界按精确消息版本确认可回收资格，`sessionSaveQueue.ts` 串行同会话保存，回收只释放内存正文和闲置客户端，再次打开沿原 Thread 读盘；身份、草稿、技能、磁盘历史与 checkpoint 不变。回归：`pnpm test:performance-retention`、`pnpm test:ui-design-pages-with-code`、`pnpm test:stage-sessions`、`pnpm test:connection-recovery`；界面验证使用 Electron。

生命周期客户端 `service/applicationLifecycle.ts` 的 Attach/Get 单次 AG-UI 状态同步请求最多等待 20 秒；超时中止客户端等待并通过原 `finally` 释放工作区共享查询，模板生成等业务动作不套用此期限。连接提示的“重新同步”在工作台和欢迎页只读取当前状态，不提交执行恢复或模板重试；预览连接同样使用独立只读 `reconnect`。`useWorkbenchRunRefresh.ts` 和手动同步按会话/请求代次丢弃迟到结果，失败不覆盖最近有效 lifecycle。回归：`pnpm test:connection-recovery` 的 `applicationLifecycleTimeout.test.ts`、`workbenchRunRefresh.test.ts`。

模板初始化的通信失败通过独立 `template_generation_failed` 前端事件保留连接错误，不再由通用 Planning `run_failed` 覆盖为 healthy；底部连接/错误兜底和欢迎页规划视图均沿用 Bootstrap 或 Template Reconcile 的专用重试。两种重试先同步后端当前资格：Bootstrap 只重试失败初始化，Template Reconcile 只提交现有 `retry_template_reconcile` 动作，不重新启动技术规划或绕过确认门。回归：`Frontend/tests/templateRecovery.test.tsx`、`Frontend/tests/applicationPlanningRuntime.test.ts`。

首次 TechnicalPlan 确认后的模板交接由 `applicationPlanningWorkflowState.ts::planningTechnicalPlanConfirmed` 联合当前完成状态、模板阶段、当前 `technicalPlanConfirmed` 确认事实与后端 `application_planning_confirmation` 产物回执判定，`ApplicationPlanningRuntime` 自动调用原 Bootstrap 编排。正式文档已外置到 `artifactRefs`，不能再依赖内联 `technical_plan.confirmation_status` 或右侧文档加载结果；仅有文档引用、待确认快照和上游旧回执不授予启动资格。回归：`pnpm test:application-planning-runtime`、`pnpm test:design-conversation`。

Current Recovery Incident ownership: `Frontend/src/renderer/src/service/recoveryActionPlan.ts` strictly parses the shared Backend `recovery-action-plan.v1`; `recoveryIncident.ts` derives the single current incident for Planning and Workbench; and `components/RecoveryIncidentCard/` owns the shared current control surface, with the Planning component and Welcome path as thin wrappers. `executionRecoveryState.ts` accepts Workbench `interrupted` or `failed` candidates only when the ActionPlan is valid and identity-matched. Workbench Recovery is also scoped to lifecycle `activeExecutions`: Ended runs are removed from lifecycle GET projections, pruned from retained frontend projections on the newer lifecycle revision, and rejected by `/execution-recovery/execute`. Browser-to-Backend health is a separate dimension owned by `service/connectionState.ts` and `components/ConnectionStatusBanner/`: transport/SSE/refresh failures never synthesize or clear a Recovery Incident, and passive reconnect only refreshes durable truth. `AiChatPanel` presents one global fallback card immediately above the bottom Composer/dock: connection failure takes visual priority over a durable Recovery Incident, and an explicit Retry first refreshes Backend truth before executing the current Backend-approved action. During an active Workbench retry or execution, `globalFallbackState.ts` suppresses the previous failure card and its error; the current failure entry returns only after the new execution settles with a recoverable failure. `MessageList` never renders generic AgentErrorCard for either current or historical messages; node-specific failure states remain. `globalFallbackState.ts::latestConversationFailure` supplies the latest message failure to the bottom surface even without a Recovery Incident, and new requests or successful messages do not inherit historical errors. Code-review retry keeps its original callback in the bottom fallback; template-generation retry remains explicit. Recoverable actions execute through `/execution-recovery/execute` with Backend-issued `incidentId` and `actionId`; `needs_attention` keeps a permanent `retry_current_failure` entry that submits only the current projected `sourceRunId`, after which Backend re-resolves the canonical lineage head and current RecoveryActionPlan. The legacy `ExecutionRecoveryCard` and frontend `/execution-recovery/run` path are removed.

Workflow failure display: a `DagPlanningError` with one issue exposes that issue's `code` through the existing Workflow `summary.errorCode`. For Workbench `UNIT_GENERATION_INFRASTRUCTURE_FAILURE`, `MessageList` suppresses the duplicate upper history error card; `recoveryIncident.ts` shows a concise model setup/invoke failure only when the failed Workflow run ID matches the current durable Recovery source and the structured code is present. Other message errors and Planning presentation keep their existing owners. Workbench `RecoveryActionPlan.primaryAction.targetNode` carries the Backend-verified retry entry only for display; raw recovery diagnostics stay hidden from the current Workbench card. The Backend ActionPlan still owns retry authority.

Planning Runtime ownership: `Frontend/src/renderer/src/service/applicationPlanningRuntime.ts` owns independent AG-UI execution, authoritative reconcile, submit/save/revision/retry/stop and temporary streaming subscriptions. Its exclusive transport primitive remains the only application-planning Graph-write owner. `applicationPlanningRecovery.ts` is the dedicated read-only AG-UI control-plane client with bounded timeout/retry and strictly parses `application-planning-recovery.v1`, including optional safe `failureDiagnostic` fields; Runtime single-flights those reads and atomically commits Workflow, lifecycle, and recovery through `reconcile_received`. Retry always reconciles first: a recoverable plan executes the Backend-issued `incidentId`/`actionId`, while `needs_attention` submits `retry_current_failure` for Backend re-resolution; neither path resends the original answer/request. `awaiting_user`, running, conflict, and unverified states remain non-retryable. Authoritative `RUN_ERROR` settles the write transport, records the failure, then performs one post-terminal reconcile so a failed Native child immediately exposes the Backend-resolved current recovery head. `applicationPlanningRecovery.ts` feeds diagnostic-only fields into the current Incident presentation; ActionPlan status and action identity remain Backend-owned. `applicationPlanningWorkflowState.ts` requires a projected Native Interrupt for current user interaction. `activeApplicationPlanning.ts` keeps business `error` separate from `syncError`, stores the recovery projection, and blocks mutations while reconciling or uncertain. Stateless interaction, checkpoint identity and bounded-stop helpers live in `applicationPlanningRuntimeHelpers.ts`, while `applicationPlanningProductConversation.ts` owns the pure product-conversation interaction/error domain functions and the ChatComposer path only re-exports them for compatibility. `hooks/useActiveApplicationPlannings.ts` owns the synchronous Canonical State getter/reducer, cold loading, visibility and template orchestration; it holds no component execution handlers. Runtime updates Canonical State before publishing recovered Workflow history. No new frontend storage contract is added.

`AppEntryPage.tsx` mounts `hooks/useApplicationPlanningRuntimes.ts` once to manage Runtime instances by application plus thread, dispose removed/replaced instances, and route Workbench submit/save/revision/retry/stop actions directly. A design-stage formal revision ensures the current stage Runtime synchronously, without a Modal-mount wait. `hooks/useApplicationPlanningWorkbenchBridge.ts` retains `deliverPlanningChunk`, current-thread history buffering, and the existing Workbench revision-continuation bridge; it is not a Planning state owner.

Only the actually visible `ApplicationPagePlanningModal.tsx` is mounted. It consumes current state, temporary streaming content, template loading and business actions; `Welcome/applicationPlanningPresentation.ts` holds its pure progress/page-count projections. `hooks/useApplicationPlanningStreamingContent.ts` subscribes the visible view to Runtime presentation content without storing tokens in Canonical State. WorkbenchPage/LeftPanel pass the `onSavePlanningRequirementSpec` business action to AiChatPanel; Runtime saves and updates current state, while the view only updates its document cache and toast from `RequirementSpecDraftSaveResult`. Focused cases A-M live in `Frontend/tests/applicationPlanningRuntime.test.ts`, with J-M covering shared pre-submit ownership, busy state, internal recovery retry and Canonical-before-submit ordering. `pnpm test:application-planning-runtime` uses the repository's Vite temporary-runner pattern. Consumer integration has not been validated because the user explicitly requested no verification.

The product and technical brand is **DevAgent Studio**. User-visible labels use the spaced name; machine identifiers use `devagentstudio`, TypeScript/Python symbols use `DevAgentStudio`, environment variables use `DEVAGENTSTUDIO_*`, and workspace artifacts use `.devagentstudio`. The Electron bridge is `window.devAgentStudio`, and the packaged backend executable is `devagentstudio-backend`.

| Path                                                                                                          | Responsibility                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Read first / common edits                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `Frontend/src/main/index.ts`                                                                                  | Electron main process: app/window/auth/preview lifecycle, storage and workspace IPC, unified controlled application deletion, template clone/retry, local sessions, and planning-artifact inspection. `applications.json` is strictly an index of `{ id, workspaceRoot, name, lastOpenedAt }`; `workspace:read-application` and `workspace:write-application` respectively read and update the sole `.devagentstudio/application.json` source, with real writes advancing `configRevision`. Application deletion is independent of the current design/planning/development stage: index-only removal stays in the renderer, while the main process requires `applicationId` plus `workspaceRoot`, completes and verifies backend deletion preparation itself, then performs protected-path/project-marker checks before trashing sessions and the managed project. `sessions:create` is the only current-contract chat-session creation path: it generates the AG-UI Thread, persists workflow and phase/stage identity without artifact ownership, and allocates DESIGN/PLAN/DEVELOPMENT sequence under the main-process lock; when a consumed revision continuation has a real running/failed/stopped lifecycle execution but its local DEVELOPMENT record is missing, the same path can recover only that execution's validated Thread through `recoveryExecutionRunId`. `sessions:save` can update content but rejects phase or thread identity drift. Current inspection uses `workbenchPlanningArtifacts.ts` to validate artifacts independently, projects pages from current ProductPlan and API/entity outlines from current TechnicalPlan (including field summaries/design status); valid pending plans remain browsable with `planningUnconfirmed`, `catalogIssues` distinguishes read failure from true empty arrays, unused `hasPageDesigns` scanning is removed, while readiness still requires confirmation and unavailable TechnicalPlan does not hide ProductPlan pages; page/API design status comes from formal planning artifacts rather than chat-session existence. | Read with `Frontend/src/main/stageSessions.ts` and `planningArtifactStatus.ts` when changing Electron lifecycle/IPC, stage-session allocation, template download, planning inspection, endpoint design-document status, app/session storage, preview, or filesystem-backed behavior. |
| `Frontend/src/main/stageSessions.ts`                                                                          | Current three-stage session primitives: product/planning/development mapping, per-Workflow/Stage sequence allocation, and an in-process serialized creation lock. It does not assign sessions to workflow/page/API/entity artifacts, create sessions for test/review/acceptance, or react to top-bar browsing. | Read with main-process `sessions:create`, renderer `service/chatSessions.ts`, and `AiChatPanel/hooks/useChatSessions.ts` when changing StageSession identity, sequence, or creation idempotency. |
| `Frontend/src/main/planningArtifactStatus.ts`                                                                 | Filesystem-backed endpoint design status: resolves the current `.devagentstudio/plans/endpoints/endpoint--<contractId>--<endpointId>.md` path and reports designed only for a non-empty Markdown document. | Read when changing workbench API design status or endpoint document naming. |
| `Frontend/src/main/filesystem.ts`                                                                            | Small Electron main-process filesystem helpers for idempotent `lstat`/recursive directory removal: an absent path is a successful no-op, while permission, type, symlink, and other filesystem errors remain visible to the caller. | Read when changing safe project deletion or other main-process filesystem operations that need explicit `ENOENT` semantics. |
| `Frontend/src/main/applicationSettings.ts`                                                                    | Versioned application-wide settings persisted atomically at Electron `app.getPath('userData')/settings.json`; currently owns the light/dark appearance theme, validates IPC writes, and broadcasts changes to every open Electron window. | Read when changing the application settings schema, theme persistence/defaults, settings IPC, or cross-window synchronization. |
| `Frontend/src/main/auth.ts`                                                                                   | Environment-scoped desktop authentication state: CMB Device Flow placeholder, current-process `auth.json` `{ access_token }` persistence, main-process memory access, and shared startup/reauthentication/quit cleanup. Cold starts delete any residual file before backend startup; ordinary window close only hides the window.                                                | Read when changing login, token persistence/memory semantics, environment auth paths, cold-start policy, or logout behavior.                                                            |
| `Frontend/src/main/backendService.ts`                                                                         | Packaged Windows/macOS backend service lifecycle: locating the PyInstaller executable, checking the generic built-in-skills resource directory, selecting a local port, health polling, renderer base URL, and process shutdown. Backend Python remains the authority for required skill names and files.        | Read when changing bundled backend startup, packaged resource structure, ports, health checks, or quit cleanup.                                                                         |
| `Frontend/src/main/designRuntimeProtocol.ts`, `designRuntimePath.ts`, `Frontend/src/renderer/src/components/DesignRenderer/`, `Frontend/src/renderer/public/design-runtime/` | Dedicated Electron design-preview asset origin: serves only the frame and prebuilt runtime from source or asar, while the renderer sends compiled TSX over a nonce-bound message channel and the frame mounts it without parent DOM access. Source-mode Electron uses a separate `userData` directory before acquiring its single-instance lock so an installed app cannot silently replace the development window. | Read with `Frontend/src/main/index.ts` startup and `Frontend/src/renderer/index.html` CSP before changing design preview packaging, iframe isolation, or runtime assets; test both Vite and packaged `file://` launches. |
| `Frontend/tsconfig.node.json`                                                                                 | TypeScript project for Electron configuration, main-process, and preload sources; loads `electron-vite/node` declarations for resource queries such as `?asset`.                                                                                                       | Keep `src/main/**/*` and `src/preload/**/*` included so `pnpm typecheck` validates desktop runtime code and resolves Electron Vite asset modules.                                        |
| `Frontend/src/preload/index.ts` and `Frontend/src/preload/index.d.ts`                                         | Context-bridge API exposed as `window.devAgentStudio` plus preload typings, including authentication status/login/token access/reauthentication, application-settings load/theme-save/change subscription, session workspace history calls, browser/report actions, and project-preview registration. | Update with every new or changed Electron IPC channel. |
| `Frontend/src/renderer/src/window.d.ts`                                                                       | Renderer-side `window.devAgentStudio` type declarations.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | Update with preload API changes.                                                                                                                                                       |
| `Frontend/src/renderer/src/main.tsx`                                                                          | React renderer entry; mounts the global authentication-failure gate outside the workbench provider so every product page receives the same blocking 401/403 UI.                                                                                                                                                 | Read for app bootstrapping or global authentication-failure UI issues.                                                                                                                 |
| `Frontend/src/renderer/src/login.tsx` and `login.less`                                                        | Dedicated frameless Electron authentication window: custom close control, draggable themed security-workbench layout, Device Flow entry, login loading/error states, and technology-oriented system/terminal presentation.                                                                                       | Read with `Frontend/src/main/index.ts` when changing login interaction, window frame/dimensions, authentication messaging, or light/dark visual treatment.                             |
| `Frontend/src/renderer/src/pages/AppEntryPage.tsx`                                                            | Top-level renderer routing between welcome flow and workbench.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Read when changing app open/create lifecycle.                                                                                                                                          |
| `Frontend/src/renderer/src/pages/WelcomePage.tsx`                                                             | Thin composition page for the welcome hero and create/open application actions.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | Read with `components/Welcome/` when changing the welcome screen layout.                                                                                                               |
| `Frontend/src/renderer/src/components/Welcome/`                                                               | Welcome and new-application planning UI. Creation requires a new/empty independent project directory, writes lifecycle state, keeps the platform database default hidden, conditionally collects one external database mode, encrypts stored credentials, and renders RequirementSpec/ProductPlan/UI/TechnicalPlan confirmation and retry states. The creation form also collects the application's remote branch name (default `dev`, validated against Git ref rules) alongside the legacy version number; before creating anything it checks the remote for that branch and, when it already exists, requires explicit user confirmation that its code will be overwritten, recording that consent as `branchOverwriteConfirmed`. | Read the creation form, planning modal, summaries, constants, template service, and credential helpers when changing initialization. |
| `Frontend/src/renderer/src/service/applicationBranches.ts` | Owns the application branch model: an application has many branches, `application.json`'s `branchName` points at the one being developed, and every other branch is read-only history. Holds `currentBranch`/`findBranch`/`isViewingHistoricalBranch`/`isBranchPublishable` plus the two invariants carried over from the version model — `resolveBranchChain` (disk is authoritative; memory only fills in when disk has no branch table) and `mergeBranches` (memory may only **add** branches, never drop disk ones, because a stale in-memory snapshot silently erasing written branches was a real production incident). | Read with `pages/WorkbenchPage.tsx` (`persistApplicationConfig` merges through `mergeBranches`), `components/VersionActions.tsx`, `components/WorkbenchVersionModals.tsx`, and `components/Welcome/applicationService.ts` before changing branch persistence or the read-only gating. |
| `Frontend/src/renderer/src/pages/AppEntryPage.tsx` | Root renderer surface switcher and Planning Runtime owner for welcome, background execution, template readiness, and the single application workbench. It mounts at most one visible planning view and routes business actions directly to the Runtime manager. A design-stage formal revision first transfers current server context to its independent stage thread; it does not wait for a Modal handler. Returning to welcome immediately releases the local preview-repair marker and fires the non-blocking Backend `leave` cleanup; opening another application never waits for preview shutdown. Electron retains its registered workspace as a final quit-time stop safeguard. Ordinary planning/workflow background isolation remains unchanged. | Read with `hooks/useApplicationPlanningRuntimes.ts`, `hooks/useApplicationPlanningWorkbenchBridge.ts`, lifecycle/template services, `ApplicationPagePlanningModal.tsx`, and `useWorkflowConversation.ts` when changing application opening, planning-to-workbench handoff, or design-revision routing. |
| `Frontend/src/renderer/src/pages/WorkbenchPage.tsx`                                                           | Workbench shell; reads the page outline and page tree from current ProductPlan `pages` through Electron inspection, hydrates persisted development status independently of TechnicalPlan confirmation, delegates directory reading to `useWorkbenchPlanningCatalog.ts` for fresh reads on re-entry/visibility, planning changes and manual refresh, without window-focus reads, with separate loading/error/retry state and per-source late-response isolation, optionally synchronizes workspace `application.json`, starts the current frontend preview server, registers successful preview workspaces with Electron for explicit-exit cleanup, and keeps its runtime origin/error in memory for page-level preview URL composition. All workflow phases remain in this workbench and use StageSession/thread boundaries rather than separate Electron phase windows. | Read when changing workbench page selection, menu hierarchy projection, workspace-backed application synchronization, phase entry, or frontend preview startup/registration state. |
| `Frontend/src/renderer/src/components/AiChatPanel/`                                                           | Main Workflow chat UI split by responsibility. Design owns RequirementSpec/ProductPlan/UiDesign; Planning owns TechnicalPlan, template preparation and controlled revision handoff; Development owns target-bound work, DAG Planning and Build. Page/API work enters `api_design_readiness_gate`, while legacy EntitySourceBinding remains an independent flow. `applicationOwnership.ts` keeps DAG lock candidates phase-strict, enriches lifecycle candidates from `ownerSessionId` then active local `executionThreadId` evidence, and retains visible-thread fallback only as a last resort. `AiChatPanel.tsx` consumes the canonical `planningState`, keeps non-owner sessions read-only, and suppresses LockDock when the visible session still has an active local Workflow entry so Debug/End controls remain available. `MessageList` uses history workflows for display while central Planning sync/mutation guards remain authoritative; the same shell preserves phase-owned sessions, test/review/acceptance handoffs, reports, code changes, preview, formal revision controls, and light/dark behavior. | Read `AiChatPanel.tsx`, `applicationOwnership.ts`, `hooks/sessionRuntime.ts`, `hooks/revisionSession.ts`, `components/ApplicationRevisionCard/`, `components/DocPanel/`, `components/PlanExecutionDock/`, `components/MessageList/`, `components/WorkflowRunCard/`, `components/SessionExecutionLockDock.tsx`, `stageOutputState.ts`, and `useWorkflowConversation.ts` when changing lifecycle locking, revision routing, DAG progress/confirmation, sessions, reports, or preview controls. |
| `Frontend/src/renderer/src/components/AiChatPanel/components/WorkflowRunCard/BuildTaskPlanConfirmation.tsx` + `BuildTaskPlanConfirmationContent.tsx` | 只读任务确认卡合并开发目标与范围，并显示页面验收及关联接口。`reviewTasks` 沿用后端顺序，默认收起，卡头仅展示序号与业务标题。`buildTaskPresentation.ts` 按路径合并文件说明，仅移除与生成器拼接完全一致的重复任务说明，目录和通配符独立列为允许修改范围。`BuildTaskFileList.tsx` 与同名 LESS 在文件名下始终展示可换行的完整路径，折叠态仅显示操作、名称和路径，展开后显示完整说明，适配明暗主题。“涉及接口”“依赖任务”“文件改动”“待验收”区域独立折叠并默认收起，涉及接口仅在有覆盖接口时展示。标题显示数量与展开/收起提示，使用淡紫分区、图标底座、操作数量徽标及细边框强化层次，文件以独立卡片和留白区分，新增绿色、修改橘黄色，空项展开后显示提示。收起区域保留内部文件详情展开状态，依赖仅展示可解析的真实名称，与待验收共用圆点列表。涉及接口按彩色方法标签、接口名称、路径同行排列并支持换行，名称取覆盖接口的 summary，缺失时提示未提供。页面与任务标准均用中性圆点。保留无关累计任务摘要及身份绑定的 AG-UI `confirm`、`abandon`、`regenerate`，Regenerate 明示旧 Pending 先被丢弃且失败不恢复。 | Read with `useWorkflowConversation.ts`, `typings/workflow.ts`, backend `protocols/workflow/request.py`, `protocols/workflow/run_control.py`, and `graph/nodes/tasks.py` when changing task-confirmation UX, target/check projection, or action payloads. |
| `Frontend/src/renderer/src/components/AiChatPanel/components/WorkflowRunCard/ExternalApiDesignPanel.tsx` + `useExternalApiDraft.ts` | Full-width vertical external API EntitySourceBinding designer. | `Frontend/src/renderer/src/components/DataSourcesPage/`                                                   | DataSourcesDrawer is the workspace entry: the database and external API entries are mutually exclusive; database connections and selected tables appear in database tabs, while external API domains are created first and then appear as domain tabs with a flat Operation list and adjacent detail drawer. `ExternalApisPane.tsx` owns the flat list projection. Directory controls are currently hidden in the workspace flow; new Operations are stored in the active domain's server-created `默认目录`, while the existing domain/directory/Operation storage contract remains unchanged. Selecting an existing Operation opens the read-only `DataSourceOperationDetails.tsx` view; explicit editing is required before the embedded editor opens. Operation creation is a two-step flow: basic information plus selectable configuration modules, followed by only the selected modules. Edit mode derives configured modules from non-empty Path/Query/Header arrays or request/response content; the top-level request parameters card contains only Path/Query/Header, while request and response bodies remain independent cards. Empty optional modules are omitted, and new empty modules cannot be saved. Request/response JSON uses the shared structure/sample tab component; editing previews follow the current sample; saving submits complete standard JSON Schema fragments containing field types and descriptions. Removed fields are cleaned on save. Domain and operation editing use themed forms, passwords stay masked, and all persistence goes through the independent AG-UI client. Path/Query editors use stable draft row IDs, independent parameter arrays, and explicit type selectors. JSON type selection preserves convertible values, updates all matching sample elements, and confirms destructive container changes before committing the draft. Request/response structures remain independent; save merging reads the latest full source so summaries cannot overwrite sibling Operations. | Read with `DataSourceEditorModal.tsx`, `DataSourceOperationDetails.tsx`, `DataSourceOperationModal.tsx`, `DataSourceOperationConfiguration.tsx`, `ExternalApisPane.tsx`, `JsonStructureViewer.tsx`, `JsonStructureViewer.less`, `service/dataSources.ts`, and `typings/dataSources.ts` when changing the data-source menu or forms. |

Domain shared headers retain their separate fixed-value editor. | Read with `ExternalApiFormParts.tsx`, `DataSourceOperationModal.tsx`, and data-source typings. |

Stop allows a short natural stream drain, then requires the backend final cancellation state, aborts residual local transport with bounded cleanup, and retains unresolved server identity after timeout so the same thread cannot start concurrently. | Read when changing workflow/conversation/recovery inputs, DAG progress, revision ordering, cancellation cleanup, failure handling, or session identity. |

| `Frontend/src/renderer/src/service/chatSessions.ts`                                                           | Renderer current chat-session contract and persistence client. A DESIGN/PLAN/DEVELOPMENT ChatSession is the StageSession itself and requires `workflowId`, mapped `stage`, global per-stage `sequence`, immutable `entryKey`/Thread, but has no workflow/page/API/entity artifact ownership. New sessions go through Electron `sessions:create`, while saves only update an already-created record. Workflow messages store their run/thread identity, scalar summary, compact events and single presentation state directly in chat JSON. `sessionWorkflowArtifacts.ts` externalizes only edited formal-document bodies; there is no independent presentation manifest or `viewRef`. Tail pagination reads only the selected message records and never loads artifact bodies for history display. Renderer derives card summaries from the single public `state`; there is no duplicate Workflow `result`. Message normalization preserves explicit revision receipts and the entity-completion `developmentContinuation` action card; the latter carries a one-shot run target and is not session ownership. A missing revision DEVELOPMENT record may pass only a lifecycle-validated `recoveryExecutionRunId`, never an arbitrary Thread. Top-bar phase browsing does not call this creation path. | Read with `AiChatPanel/hooks/revisionSession.ts`, `hooks/sessionRuntime.ts`, `hooks/useChatSessions.ts`, `Frontend/src/main/stageSessions.ts`, main-process session normalization, and `docs/APPLICATION_REVISION_WORKFLOW.md` when changing chat history/session behavior, phase ownership, formal-revision recovery, or persisted message shape. |
| `Frontend/src/renderer/src/service/applicationDeletion.ts`, `applicationStorage.ts`, `applicationConfigOperation.ts` | AG-UI clients for deletion and application configuration, plus application-index load/save/removal. Settings sends managed field changes through `/application-config/run`, refreshes canonical application.json, and reports actual `.env`/YAML changes and restart needs. `applicationStorage` persists only `{ id, workspaceRoot, name, lastOpenedAt }` in the index. The Electron write IPC rejects changes to generated applications' managed config fields; other existing application metadata still uses the established save path. Full deletion uses the existing backend fence and OS trash flow. | Read with `components/Welcome/applicationService.ts`, `components/SettingsPage/`, `WelcomeRecentProjects.tsx`, `chatSessions.ts`, Electron `src/main/index.ts`, and Backend configuration/deletion protocols when changing saved applications or client recovery. |
| `Frontend/src/renderer/src/components/ProjectPlanPageTreePreview/`                                            | Shared menu-tree preview component that converts `ProjectPlan.frontend_pages` into a visual hierarchy, including menu `unique_path` and leaf-page descriptions. The ProjectPlan confirmation surface reuses it inside a JSON-driven modular summary; Markdown remains the formal artifact but does not decide which confirmation modules are displayed. | Read before changing ProjectPlan confirmation modules, menu preview rendering, or tree-node conversion helpers. |
| `Frontend/src/renderer/src/service/applicationSettings.ts` and `hooks/useApplicationTheme.tsx`                | Renderer application-theme client and root Provider. It loads Electron `userData/settings.json` once before initial render, keeps theme state shared across welcome/planning/workbench/authentication surfaces, receives cross-window updates, and uses memory-only behavior in non-Electron browser development without localStorage fallback. | Read when changing application-wide appearance settings, initial theme loading, theme persistence, or cross-window theme behavior. |
| `Frontend/src/renderer/src/service/applicationLifecycle.ts`, `templateApi.ts`, `activeApplicationPlanning.ts`, and `applicationPagePlanning.ts` | Dedicated lifecycle AG-UI client, plus the in-memory active-planning view and creation-planning Graph client. It sends only Backend-owned lifecycle actions; `bootstrap_template_generation` never receives repository URLs, branch/commit facts, download results, manifests, or completion results from Electron. Renderer lifecycle reads merge concurrent requests by Workspace and execute Attach → Get. Lifecycle Get additionally returns read-only `extensions.workbenchProgress`, projected by `services/workbench_progress_projection.py` from current executions and exact Run/Thread-matched PlanningRun snapshots using the existing DAG progress contract. `AiChatPanel/workbenchRunPresentation.ts` restores owner-session messages and `hooks/useWorkbenchRunRefresh.ts` refreshes only detached running executions through the existing AG-UI lifecycle client; all displayed loading/debug controls share that execution state, while stop uses the existing Run control. Application-list restoration, opening, workbench mounting, and Renderer restart use this path. Attach preserves active Bootstrap, cleans orphaned GENERATING workspaces, and keeps GENERATING on cleanup failure; Backend get stays strictly read-only. Business stages come directly from the projected lifecycle; no old localStorage, application-index, or checkpoint stage migration is supported. | Read when changing creation planning confirmation, lifecycle AG-UI payloads, Bootstrap/Attach behavior, homepage status, or workbench entry gating. |
| `Frontend/src/renderer/src/service/workspaceTools.ts`                                                         | Frontend client helpers for backend workspace tools and approval approve/reject calls.                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Read when changing workspace tool invocation or approval API usage from the UI.                                                                                                        |
| `Frontend/src/renderer/src/context/WorkbenchContext.tsx`                                                      | Shared workbench state for assistant messages published to editor panels.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | Read when changing cross-panel message flow.                                                                                                                                           |

It intentionally does not duplicate Backend skill manifests.                       | Read when changing frontend desktop packaging or packaged resource structure.                                                                                                           |

## Generated Or External-Like Areas

| Path                                                        | Notes                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `Frontend/node_modules/`, `Frontend/out/`, `Frontend/dist/` | Generated/dependency output; do not inspect for normal feature work.                                                                                                                                           |
| `Frontend/resources/backend/`                               | Generated platform backend staging output from `scripts/build-backend-win.ps1` and `scripts/build-backend-mac.sh`; contains PyInstaller support files and only the selected environment configuration file. Do not commit or inspect secrets.     |
| `Backend/build/`, `Backend/dist/`                           | Generated PyInstaller build output.                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Backend/.venv/`, `Backend/.pycache/`                       | Environment/cache output; do not inspect for normal feature work.                                                                                                                                              |
| `var/workspaces/`                                           | Runtime workflow workspace artifacts such as generated specs, plans, JSON `build-task-plan.json` / `repair-task-plan.json`, reports, previews, and other local run output. Default workflow writes resolve here from the repository root. Do not treat as source unless a task explicitly asks to inspect a run artifact. |
| `.pnpm-store/`                                              | Package store; do not inspect.                                                                                                                                                                                 |

## Common Change Routes

| Task                                                   | Start here                                             | Usually also touch                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Add or change backend API route                        | `Backend/app/main.py`                                  | Runtime/tool module, frontend service caller, validation docs if behavior changes.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Add or change workspace/file/git/terminal tool         | `Backend/app/workspace/workspace.py`                   | `Backend/app/main.py`, `Backend/app/tools/*` when exposed to DeepAgents, affected `Backend/app/agents/*`, frontend tool UI/service if exposed.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Change app-generation workflow phase order/routing     | `docs/WORKFLOW.md`, `Backend/app/graph/workflow.py`    | `Backend/app/graph/state.py`, matching `graph/nodes/*`, `graph/subgraphs/unit_testing.py`, `graph/subgraphs/testing.py`, `graph/subgraphs/acceptance.py`, `protocols/workflow/`, `protocols/workflow_visualization.py`, frontend `workbenchPhase.ts`, `WorkbenchTopBar.tsx`, `MessageList`, `WorkflowRunCard`, `AiChatPanel` acceptance dock, and workflow debug UI when node selection changes. The current workbench order is design → development (Build → unit_test → unit_test_repair) → test (integration/repair/review confirmation) → review (code review → acceptance phase confirmation) → acceptance (launch → acceptance review → finalize), with separate unit-test/test-phase/review/acceptance confirmations and independent development/test/review/acceptance sessions. |
| Change workflow state or persisted artifact references | `Backend/app/graph/state.py`                           | Matching node, `workspace/*_documents.py`, `domain/models.py`, `workflow.md` if design semantics change.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Change requirement confirmation in workflow            | `Backend/app/graph/nodes/requirements.py`              | `agents/main/requirements_analyzer.py`, `services/requirement_spec.py`, `services/entity_definitions.py`, `tools/ask_user.py`, and `workspace/spec_documents.py`.
