### Preview runtime maintenance

- `services/frontend_node_runtime.py` 统一解析用户工程 Node 与包管理器环境，供预览、历史版本、受控 pnpm 安装和集成/性能检查使用。Windows 从 `NVM_SYMLINK` 锁定真实 Node；macOS 使用继承的 `NVM_BIN`；未配置 NVM 时按继承的 PATH 解析。包管理器脚本环境核对 Node 的路径和完整版本；启动日志和测试执行记录保存来源。工作区内安装与启动/构建共用前端操作锁。当前进程及安装记录分别保存在 `runtime/launch/frontend-node.json` 与 `runtime/launch/frontend-dependencies-node.json`，仅身份匹配时复用。回归：`tests.test_frontend_node_runtime`、`tests.test_project_launcher`、`tests.test_integration_test_runner`、`tests.test_frontend_performance_runner`。

- 服务状态卡片通过 `/preview-runtime/run` 的 `stop_frontend` / `stop_backend` 及当前 `attemptId` 独立停止标准预览的一端。启动器在工作区启动锁内校验尝试身份，保留另一端和本轮日志，失败不提交停机状态。持久化占用记录保留原尝试，进程重启后只收口同一尝试的目标服务。卡片用 `stoppedByUser` 区分手动停止，前端停止时收起旧预览，已发布版本切换标签时不自动拉起已手动停止的服务。运行快照共用跨平台 PID 存活探测。回归：`tests.test_preview_runtime`、`pnpm test:preview-runtime`、`pnpm test:released-version`。

- Java 后端启动与 `preview_runtime_state.py` 的实时校准共用 `backend_project_launcher.py::_backend_startup_status`：仅本轮 `Started <Application> in ... seconds` 代表启动完成，版本横幅与端口初始化无效；双流启动失败/端口占用优先，即使进程仍存活也投影 failed、清除 ready/previewUrl 并开放原诊断入口。回归：`tests.test_project_launcher`、`tests.test_preview_runtime`。

- `Backend/app/protocols/preview_runtime.py` owns `/preview-runtime/run`: get/watch/restart/stop/diagnose/confirm/revise/cancel/leave use standard AG-UI lifecycles. Entering the workbench never starts services; only the service drawer's manual `restart` starts the standard backend/frontend. `leave` releases maintenance synchronously, returns immediately, and owns a background double-stop that closes frontend/backend before and after any cancelled worker finishes. Electron records manually restarted workspaces and stops them again before desktop quit. A persisted restart/stop owner without a matching in-process job is reconciled as an interrupted orphan on the next read or operation: partial services are stopped, the attempt becomes directly retryable without diagnosis, and maintenance is released. `services/preview_runtime_state.py` maintains the current standard-preview attempt, live PID/readiness checks, redacted bounded frontend/backend logs, and the actual frontend/backend service ports. The mounted workbench keeps a reconnecting lightweight status subscription without logs so the toolbar and drawer share the same live state; opening the drawer enables bounded log transfer.
- `services/preview_runtime_guard.py` serializes preview operations under an atomic maintenance lock but no longer blocks Workflow registration, workspace leases, application lifecycle startup, or other ordinary application tasks. Manual `restart` and exit cleanup `stop` bypass application-task blockers; diagnose/repair still refuses active running/stopping application tasks, but a paused development execution (`awaiting_user`, such as unit-test confirmation) no longer blocks startup diagnosis or repair confirmation. Renderer `serviceStatusPolicy.ts::previewRepairExecutionBlocks` and `AiChatPanel` use the same active-execution boundary. Initialization and independent product interaction gates remain checked, and duplicate preview maintenance remains serialized. The durable current maintenance owner remains until a real worker completes or a pending repair is explicitly stopped. Regression: `tests.test_preview_runtime`, `pnpm test:preview-runtime`.
- `services/preview_runtime_repair.py` reuses RepairPlanner and SmallTask for exact-file startup repair, normalizes RepairPlanner virtual absolute paths into validated workspace-relative task scopes, confirms each Markdown plan against its current source digest, validates affected builds, and restarts standard preview. A maximum of three dispatched repair rounds is independent of test/review budgets; this flow never increments development completion or advances workbench phases.
- `domain/preview_diagnostics.py` defines the current structured preview failure contract (stage/category/message, optional parser location and command exit/timeout, bounded redacted excerpt and current log paths). `services/preview_diagnostics.py` adapts actual parser exceptions and command output without filename-specific repair rules; frontend/backend launchers produce it and `preview_runtime_state.py` persists/projects `runtime.diagnostics` through the existing AG-UI endpoint, clearing it on success/stop. `preview_repair_evidence.py` prioritizes current failed validation over the original launch error when attempt identities match, provides related source windows and verified candidate files, and renders evidence in the Markdown confirmation plan. The preview-only RepairPlanner prompt and `middleware/preview_diagnosis_scope.py` keep exploration on source and declared current logs, excluding self/history runtime records; repeated identical queries with unchanged results fail explicitly at the model boundary using invocation-local evidence hashes, without adding a model-call ceiling. Other planner scopes are unchanged. SmallTask consumes the same diagnostics and strategy, with the existing confirmation/digest/three-round gates intact. Regression: `tests.test_preview_diagnostics`, `tests.test_preview_repair_evidence`, `tests.test_preview_runtime`, `tests.test_project_launcher`, frontend `pnpm test:preview-runtime`.
- `services/preview_runtime_worker.py` binds each synchronous preview worker and nested RunnableConfig to `preview-runtime:<threadId>`. The protocol forwards cancel to the existing model/tool/process registry, projects diagnosis as running, and settles cancellation exceptions as stopped only after the real worker exits. Renderer `usePreviewRuntime` ignores aborted requests' late results and restores the stop action when cancellation fails. Regression: `tests.test_preview_runtime_cancellation` (real DeepAgent/OpenAI SDK heartbeat cancellation and safe tool cleanup), `Frontend/tests/previewRuntimeCancellation.test.tsx` via `pnpm test:preview-runtime`.
- `AiChatPanel/hooks/usePreviewRuntime.ts` owns operation lifetime, creates DEVELOPMENT history sessions with `preview-repair:` entry keys, and persists progress/results in the selected repair conversation. It keeps one reconnecting no-log status subscription while the workbench is mounted and switches to bounded log projection while the drawer is open. `BrowserPreviewPanel/ServiceStatusDrawer` replaces decorative window dots with “服务状态”, separates frontend/backend logs, and supports theme tokens. `ServiceLogContent` owns selectable log text, scroll following, a top-right search field (Enter/icon submission, literal case-insensitive highlighting and match count), and the themed context menu for copying selected text or all current-layer logs. Search pauses following and scrolls only the log pane; clearing removes highlights. `useServiceLogClipboard` uses the preload `clipboard.writeText` bridge and `src/main/clipboard.ts`'s validated `clipboard:write-text` IPC to copy through Electron's native clipboard with success/error feedback. `PreviewRepairControls` supplies the floating confirm/stop actions and formal-revision conversation handoff; stopping aborts the renderer AG-UI operation before the server cancellation settles.
- See `docs/PREVIEW_RUNTIME.md` for confirmation, cancellation, log and concurrency semantics. Regression entry points: `tests.test_preview_runtime`, `tests.test_project_launcher`, `tests.test_ag_ui_action_stream`, `tests.test_workspace_run_lease`, frontend `pnpm test:preview-runtime`, `pnpm test:service-logs`, `pnpm test:stage-sessions` and `pnpm test:preview-inspector`; verify UI in Electron.
- `AiChatPanel/hooks/usePreviewRuntimeConnection.ts` owns preview-only connection generations and read/watch reconnection; `previewRuntimeRecovery.ts` chooses bottom-entry actions after an authoritative read without replaying confirmation. Preview transport errors enter the existing bottom connection surface independently of Workflow. Orphaned diagnose/confirm/revise workers are stopped with `repair.interrupted`, preserving round budgets and changes; only these stopped sessions may use `revise` to regenerate a plan requiring fresh confirmation. Pending confirmation and live workers retain maintenance ownership.

## Validation Routes


- `docs/ELECTRON_RECOVERY_TEST_2026_10_06.md` records the ongoing real-model Electron interruption matrix, unresolved UI generation-pool recovery issue, evidence paths and remaining stages. It is an incomplete test record, not a blanket acceptance claim.

### 数据源与绑定工作台交互

- Endpoint 静态来源由 `domain/api_design_static.py` 校验 `sourceBinding.sourceType=static`、`staticData` 和静态字段路径；`FieldMapping/{StaticMapping,staticData}` 管理 JSON 应用、失效校验和返回预览。继续复用 `/endpoint-designs/run` 的草稿/确认、双文件、就绪门禁和冻结契约，不建立全局静态目录或新 API。该模式依次展示数据来源/数据内容/返回字段三张卡片；生成职责为对象、应用服务和控制器，无数据库或上游访问。回归：`tests.test_api_design_static`、`Frontend/tests/staticData.test.ts`，限制和交互见 `docs/FIELD_VALUE_RULES.md`。

- `Backend/app/services/data_sources.py` owns selected MySQL tables and AG-UI live metadata for direct and DBID connections; field structures are not stored in the source object. Both modes support connection detection and adding tables. DBID forms submit only connection fields and the DBID, while direct forms submit encrypted passwords; switching modes never submits another mode's fields. `Backend/app/services/binding_workspace.py` stores v7 per-Endpoint drafts under `.devagentstudio/drafts/endpoint-bindings/`, creates that category directory only when the user saves a draft, and validates the selected source/table at confirmation. Current source references traverse `databaseQuery`, `databaseWrites`, `externalApiBindings`, and `fieldMappings`. Connection updates preserve or clear selected tables according to the database location change; drafts never change formal artifacts before confirmation.
- `protocols/data_sources.py` / `routes/data_sources.py` add AG-UI selected_tables/add_tables/remove_tables/references actions; database table metadata also projects the read-only `columnCount` used by the add-table picker. `protocols/endpoint_designs.py` adds save_draft/discard_draft; preparation includes the current TechnicalPlan hash and saved draft. `services/endpoint_design_detail.py` serializes formal save/revision validation and draft cleanup; optional bindingSelection/technicalPlanHash enforce the simplified single-object path without removing the full editor contract.
- `Frontend/src/renderer/src/components/DataSourcesPage/DataSourcesDrawer.tsx` owns the mutually exclusive database/external-API drawers, database connection tabs, domain tabs, and adjacent detail drawer. `ExternalApisPane.tsx` renders the active domain's flat Operation list; `DataSourceOperationDetails.tsx` is the shared read-only detail view with non-empty request/response cards that can be folded independently; `DatabaseTables.tsx` owns live discovery/add/remove/read-only columns; the existing operation editor supports embedded rendering only after an explicit edit action or when creating an Operation. The external-API flow requires creating a domain before adding Operations, hides directory controls, and assigns new Operations to the active domain's `默认目录`. Connection and domain editing load full detail before saving; table identity includes Schema. Closing or switching detail cancels pending reads, and reopening always loads current data. Deleting resources queries formal references first. SessionSidebar delegates navigation to the same dirty-input guard; workspaceDialogs hosts themed confirmations.
- `Frontend/src/renderer/src/components/AiChatPanel/components/FieldMapping/` owns the persistent single-source workspace and retains the original source selection/change flow (no new database/API scenario tabs). `ValueRuleEditor`, `RuleEditor`, and `valueRules` share target value selection, modal rule editing and per-input source/field selection; missing-value controls and summaries are hidden while stored policies remain intact; `ResponseFieldMapping` supports multiple fields from the same source plus request/context dependencies. `MappingDescription` replaces per-input business usage with optional endpoint-level natural-language guidance, persisted through the existing `implementationDescription` field and consumed by planning/generation. Unapplied rule editors block outer saves. Query/write editors and `ExternalMapping` consume the same controls; external request bindings are target-owned and allow input reuse. Theme styles live in `ValueRuleEditor.less`.
- `useEndpointDesignRecovery` scopes independent mapping failures by workspace/Endpoint/reader; `bindingRecovery` synchronizes catalog, selected tables, contract and source metadata through read-only AG-UI actions. Mapping-panel retry and the full editor's “同步状态” preserve local inputs, compare formal revisions, and never replay save/discard or “确认并检测”. Server business failures remain distinct from transport loss. Mapping errors and retries stay inside the field-mapping panel or its full editor and never feed the main Workflow error/connection surface. Confirmed database mapping details render saved field snapshots without live database metadata; entering edit mode loads current columns, and read-only synchronization also skips database connections.
- `service/dataSourceEvents.ts` 在来源/表清单成功增删改后按完整工作区通知读取者；`FieldMapping/bindingSources.ts` 通过现有 AG-UI 动作只读刷新目录和已添加表，丢弃旧请求及卸载后回执。右侧来源下拉展开也刷新候选；不重新读取或替换接口草稿，不推进确认门禁。更换来源期间旧映射表单保持挂载但隐藏，取消可恢复未应用输入。回归：`pnpm test:api-design` 的 `bindingSources.test.ts`。
- The main development readiness gate still requires explicit refresh/confirmation. Saving a draft never marks an Endpoint configured; formal confirmation only updates local gate readiness and leaves Build/development completion unchanged. Regression: `tests.test_binding_workspace`, existing Endpoint/data-source/API-design suites; frontend static `pnpm typecheck:web`.

- `DataSourcesPage/DataSourceEditorModal.tsx` 的数据库配置弹窗使用 `DatabaseConnectionForm.tsx` 和 `DatabaseConnectionForm.less` 展示紧凑分组表单，连接方式仅提供数据库直连与 DBID；已有内置来源保持受管身份，不转换连接类型。数据库弹窗宽度为 650px，独立设置标题、两列连接字段、密码保留提示和低强调删除入口，复用弹窗明暗主题令牌。检测结果直接消费现有 AG-UI `DataSourceValidation`，区分真实连通与静态校验；修改输入清除旧结果，检测和保存期间锁定表单，关闭或切换来源后丢弃迟到回执。外部 API 继续使用原表单，数据源存储与后端接口保持不变。

### Endpoint API design and readiness

- AI 辅助各阶段及 Mock 的系统文案与输入编排均集中在 `agents/endpoint_design_assist_prompts.py`，不另设文案模块。系统提示按「角色与边界 / 本步任务 / 处理规则 / 取值方式（适用时）/ 待明确问题 / 输出要求」组织；用户输入按「本步任务 / 处理目标（适用时）/ 接口与操作对象 / 需求说明 / 补充说明 / 本轮前序结果 / 可用输入 / 当前表结构」按需投影。说明原文保留自然换行，返回清单前置，规则完善仅展示目标、当前说明和已选依赖；操作不传字段或 DDL，映射说明不重复传字段结构。Mock 单独使用返回目标、需求和生成条数，不继承映射提示的禁止虚构样例规则。当前步骤职责、JSON 输出、模型调用与校验合同不变；本次按用户要求不进行测试或运行验证。

- AI 映射提示中的表结构由 `services/endpoint_design_assist_ddl.py` 复用当前直连/DBID 数据源凭据，通过只读 `SHOW CREATE TABLE` 从数据库取得原始 DDL；`generate_mapping` 将原文传给查询、写入和返回步骤，不拼接或裁剪外部表定义，读取失败不回退。内置来源当前没有原始 DDL 读取合同，按用户要求暂时保留 `agents/endpoint_design_assist_prompts.py` 的元数据拼接，明确标注为非原始 DDL，空 Schema 不输出限定符。操作识别仍只接收业务概要，现有结构化元数据校验及应用逻辑不变；凭据不进入提示词。本次按用户要求不进行测试工作。

- 独立动作的阶段报告由 `protocols/ag_ui_action_stream.py` 等待当前进度的标准 Custom、状态快照及可选消息帧交给流消费者后再返回，避免 Endpoint AI 辅助已进入 operation/query 等步骤而前端仍显示上一阶段；模型文本增量仍按原队列转发。消息等待任务在每次等待的 `finally` 中取消并等待退出，覆盖业务先结束、外层取消与 ASGI 断连；关流同时收口等待交付回执的业务任务。沿用现有 AG-UI 帧与阶段字段，不增加前端阶段推断或新传输协议。回归：`tests.test_ag_ui_action_stream` 的逐帧交付顺序、`tests.test_ag_ui_action_stream_cancellation` 的任务回收与 ASGI 断连，以及 `tests.test_endpoint_design_assist_stream` 的取消生命周期。

- 查询阶段提示由 `agents/endpoint_design_assist_prompts.py::_TASKS["query"]` 独立维护，不再拼接通用映射和取值策略；保留原简洁输出、请求依赖及最多一层 AND/OR 子组。提示区分筛选与分页/排序参数、直接/固定/业务取值，限定本阶段问题，缺少会改变筛选语义的信息时整组留空提问。没有增加缺值策略、自动忽略条件或新协议。同步回归 `tests.test_endpoint_design_assist_query` 解析实际提示中的示例，经过真实 Pydantic、字段转换和组合校验，覆盖关键词 OR、分类/状态 AND 及阶段策略隔离。

- AI 映射生成的首次输入是必填需求说明：`GenerateMappingRequest.instruction` 与可选 `clarification` 分开传递，前后端均拒绝空白主需求，两段合计最多 4000 字。`mappingAssistModel.ts` 为实际请求生成独立的需求/补充文本，以结构化身份绑定过期检查；`useMappingAssist`、`MappingAssistPanel` 和审阅底栏统一阻止空需求生成。`services/endpoint_design_assist.py` 的操作调用只投影接口概要、当前表身份及两类说明，`agents/endpoint_design_assist_prompts.py` 在操作阶段不读取任何字段清单，后续映射步骤保留按区域的真实字段。仍使用 `/endpoint-designs/run`、原动作及 v7 正式映射，无新增端点或持久化字段。回归覆盖必填、补充隔离、4000 字边界、操作输入精简及 AG-UI 失败生命周期：`tests.test_endpoint_design_assist_steps`、`tests.test_endpoint_design_assist_retry`、`tests.test_endpoint_design_assist_operation`、`tests.test_endpoint_design_assist_stream` 与前端 `mappingAssistHooks/Review/Runtime.test.tsx`。

- AI 映射生成后的审阅由 `FieldMapping/MappingAssistResult.tsx`、`MappingAssistIssues.tsx`、`MappingAssistPreview.tsx` 与 `MappingAssistQueryPreview.tsx` 负责，独立于原编辑表单；`MappingAssistResult.less` 继承 `--wb-*` 浅深主题变量。`mappingAssistReviewModel.ts` 根据真实返回字段身份归并重复问题、保留无法唯一归属的问题，并按生成操作投影分区与数量。查询树共用表头保留 AND/OR，返回项可定位到字段补充框。`description` 阶段提示以 2～3 句、80 字左右概括业务用途和已配置的关键特殊规则，不逐列复述映射或夹带校验过程；未明确内容只进入 `questions`，不重复前序问题。映射说明在预览中直接完整展示，取消首句摘要和全文展开的重复呈现，继续使用原主题样式及空态；`implementationDescription` 存储与输出合同不变。`useMappingAssist` 将按字段回答和其他补充共同绑定生成基线，校验 4000 字总上限；回答需整套重新生成才可替换。底部确认列出将置空字段，原整体替换与一次撤销继续复用 `/endpoint-designs/run`，无新协议、正式格式或模型步骤。回归入口：`Frontend/tests/mappingAssistReview.test.tsx`、`mappingAssistHooks.test.tsx` 和 `mappingAssistRuntime.test.tsx`，由 `apiDesign.test.ts` 聚合；本次按用户要求不进行测试、构建、health、类型检查或 Electron 验证。

- AI 映射操作识别与精简交互：`domain/endpoint_design_assist_steps.py::OperationStepResult` 与 `agents/endpoint_design_assist_prompts.py` 增加独立 `operation` 步骤，`services/endpoint_design_assist.py` 在识别后按新 CRUD 编排；旧操作不进入识别提示。操作提示独立于通用映射策略，明确业务语义与辅助线索的边界，只对影响 CRUD 判断的歧义提问；已明确软删除的具体字段/取值留给写入步骤核对。歧义通过原 `/endpoint-designs/run` 的成功生命周期交付 `mapping=null`、`operation=null`、`operationReason` 和问题；明确结果含 `mapping.databaseOperation`，整套替换按新操作校验。临时重试缓存包含本轮已验证操作；正式 v7、原确认门禁和来源身份不变。`FieldMapping/MappingAssistPanel` 压缩输入上下文，`MappingAssistReview.tsx` 通过专用预览上下文把需核对依据放到对应字段或查询组；`DatabaseMapping` 保留原表单卡片顺序和编辑行为。回归新增 `tests.test_endpoint_design_assist_operation`；同步转换用例覆盖真实无草稿模型上下文及操作提示的策略隔离，避免转换层错误依赖 `draft`。前端 hook/runtime 用例覆盖未知操作、跨操作替换、撤销与歧义阻断；按本次要求不执行前端测试、构建、health 或 Electron 验证。

- 单表数据库 AI 辅助由 `domain/endpoint_design_assist.py`、`services/endpoint_design_assist{,_validation}.py` 和无工具 `agents/endpoint_design_assist.py` 负责；原 `/endpoint-designs/run` 增加 `generate_mapping/refine_rule/replace_mapping`。读取当前正式契约和实时表结构，映射模型不接收旧草稿，从空配置生成整套映射；未知返回项显式未配置。`generate_mapping` 按查询/写入/返回/说明自动串行调用，跳过 CRUD 不适用步骤，逐步校验、通过 AG-UI 报告阶段，完成后统一预览；失败或取消不回填部分结果。`agents/endpoint_design_assist_prompts.py` 维护独立中文短提示与可读字段清单，`domain/endpoint_design_assist_steps.py` 定义模型专用简洁输出，`services/endpoint_design_assist_steps.py` 负责权威身份回填和本轮配置摘要；模型不接收完整领域 JSON Schema。整体替换重新核验版本/草稿/元数据并返回内存草稿，无落盘及主 Workflow 推进。现有 v7 草稿和正式确认合同不变。Frontend `FieldMapping/{useMappingAssist,mappingAssistModel,MappingAssistPanel,RuleAssistSection,MappingAssist.less}` 负责整套映射预览、问题补充再生成、异步隔离、一次撤销和规则文字采用；分区审阅使用专用只读视图，页头为唯一映射生成入口，`RuleEditor` 保留原依赖和隐藏策略。数据库入口仅支持当前单表 CRUD，外部 API、静态来源由下述专用服务负责，完整复杂编辑器不开放 AI 入口。回归：`tests.test_endpoint_design_assist`、`tests.test_endpoint_design_assist_stream`，纯同步提示与转换回归 `tests.test_endpoint_design_assist_steps`；前端 `apiDesign.test.ts` 聚合辅助 model/runtime 用例。此次实施的前端测试、构建、health 和 Electron 检查按用户要求跳过，类型检查单独执行。

- 单个外部 Operation 的 AI 辅助由 `domain/endpoint_design_external_assist.py`、`services/endpoint_design_external_assist{,_validation}.py` 负责，复用无工具 Agent、`agents/endpoint_design_assist_prompts.py` 和 `/endpoint-designs/run` 的三种辅助动作；`protocols/endpoint_designs.py` 按当前 `selection.sourceType` 分派严格模型，能力元数据新增 `mappingAssistSourceTypes` 与 `externalMappingGenerationSteps`，仍消费 `endpoint-designs` 事件和 `endpointDesigns` 快照。基于当前正式应用契约和 `api_design.py::load_external_operation` 的公开字段，按 request/return/description 串行生成，不实际调用外部服务、不把认证值、样例或旧映射传给模型。入参只读应用请求依赖，返回只读当前外部响应和应用请求；校验器补齐权威快照并核验方向、类型、目标全集、隐藏策略及组合配置，未明确目标保留未配置。从失败阶段显式重试、取消、草稿/版本/结构失效和无落盘整套替换共用原边界，不改 v7 或正式确认。Frontend `ExternalMapping.tsx`、`MappingAssist{Panel,Result,Preview,Issues}.tsx`、`mappingAssistReviewModel.ts`、`useMappingAssist.ts` 与辅助类型接入请求页签、完整请求身份的问题定位、替换标记/撤销和原规则文字采用；继承现有主题样式。后端回归：`tests.test_endpoint_design_external_assist`、`tests.test_endpoint_design_external_assist_stream`；前端 `externalMappingAssist.test.tsx` 由 `apiDesign.test.ts` 聚合，继续按用户要求仅编写、不执行前端测试、构建、health 或 Electron 验证。

- 静态 AI 辅助由 `domain/endpoint_design_static_assist.py`、`services/endpoint_design_static_assist{,_validation}.py` 负责，复用原映射三动作和 Agent，新增独立 `generate_mock_data/apply_mock_data` AG-UI 动作；`endpoint_designs` 能力声明包括静态 return 步骤、Mock 步骤和最多 20 条的规模。映射只读当前已应用静态字段与应用请求，模型不接收旧值或旧规则；整体替换只改返回映射，严格核对静态数据未变化。Mock 只读正式返回契约与需求，校验精确路径全集、类型、每组列表数量、同构结构、256 KiB/12 层及安全字段/数值，应用只替换内存数据并保留原映射供核对。Frontend `StaticMapping.tsx` 接入页头映射能力、规则完善、独立 Mock 按钮及应用展开，`useMockDataAssist.ts` / `StaticMockDataPanel.tsx` / `.less` 管理标准客户端调用、候选、取消、过期阻断、互斥、一次撤销和主题；静态 JSON 输入按已应用内容绑定编辑基线，避免回填时瞬间 dirty 错误失效撤销。提示仍集中于 `agents/endpoint_design_assist_prompts.py`，不新建文案文件，不改 v7、IPC、持久化来源或主流程确认门。按用户本次指示不新增测试用例，不运行测试、类型检查、构建、health、Python 编译或 Electron 验证，由用户手动验证。

- AI 映射辅助优化：`services/endpoint_design_assist_retry.py` 只在进程内短期保存本轮已验证前序步骤；`generate_mapping.retryToken` 在基线与要求一致时一次性恢复失败阶段，标准失败结果通过 `assistFailure` 提供阶段和凭据，不新增端点或自动重放。后续提示读取本轮已完成摘要，真实主键/自增/可空/默认值参与提示及元数据摘要，未知约束不猜测；固定值/业务处理需简短依据，`reviews` 只在辅助预览展示。Frontend `useMappingAssist` 同时比较用户要求和问题回答，旧结果可查看但不可替换；`useRuleAssist.ts` 管理规则候选，原规则弹窗持有外层保存锁。同步回归新增 `tests.test_endpoint_design_assist_retry`，聚合前端入口新增真实 React hook 的 `mappingAssistHooks.test.tsx`、`ruleAssistHooks.test.tsx`，共用 `mappingAssistTestHarness.tsx` 的标准 AG-UI 假服务。正式 v7、保存确认和主工作流门禁不变。

- `Backend/app/services/api_design_schema.py` resolves local references and `allOf` for API-design leaf enumeration, merging nested properties and required constraints. `business_acceptance.py` keeps TechnicalPlan entity semantics separate from Endpoint field mappings, while projecting one-sentence business descriptions to Controller/ApplicationService checks.
- `Frontend/.../WorkflowRunCard/ApiBusinessDescriptionEditor.tsx` edits one concise sentence for either a Request or Response field. `apiDesignTableModel.ts` and `apiDesignSerialization.ts` keep `source_mapping`/`business_description` records, source cleanup, and validation pure; draft-only `unconfigured` rows cannot be confirmed and no executable expression is persisted.
- `Backend/app/graph/nodes/api_design.py` owns the `api_design_readiness_gate` used by page/API development. Any target with Endpoint dependencies first projects the full `apiDesigns` list, including confirmed items for view/edit; `refresh` rechecks the missing subset and every displayed revision before continuing. `Backend/app/services/endpoint_design_detail.py` and `Backend/app/protocols/endpoint_designs.py` provide independent AG-UI `get/prepare/save/save_draft/discard_draft` actions; save validates the complete mapping, checks `baseRevision`, and writes the JSON/Markdown pair without changing the workspace Formal Build DAG. The retained EntitySourceBinding path is legacy-only and is not a prerequisite for normal Build.
- `services/api_design_values.py` validates request dependency snapshots and projects full rule summaries. `endpoint_design_documents.py`, `EndpointDesignResult`, frozen contract source indexes, Data Source generation, and business acceptance consume v7 rules without losing dependencies or missing policies. `ApplicationOutline` and `planningArtifactStatus.ts` accept only v7 formal pairs. Regression: `tests.test_api_design_values`, `tests.test_binding_workspace`, and the existing API-design suites.
- `Backend/app/protocols/endpoint_designs.py` and `Backend/app/services/endpoint_design_detail.py` expose the independent `/endpoint-designs/run` AG-UI `get/prepare/save/save_draft/discard_draft` actions. `Frontend/src/renderer/src/service/endpointDesigns.ts` consumes its lifecycle and `EndpointDesignResult` renders the same formal JSON projection in the right-side artifact detail and the readiness gate. The gate lists every target Endpoint with its current status (`confirmed`/`pending`/`stale`) whenever the target has Endpoint dependencies, lets each item open the right-side workspace for viewing or configuration, and keeps the workflow “确认并检测” action for explicit re-detection only. The gate does not persist an aggregate mapping snapshot; completion, stop, failure, or later mapping edits do not change the current Endpoint design contract.

### Entity binding development continuation

- `Backend/app/services/development_continuation.py` owns the server-recorded original page/API target, binding validation and readiness/token issuance. Request parsing validates without consumption; `services/application_lifecycle.py::start_workbench_execution` atomically consumes the token and replaces the original execution.
- `Backend/app/graph/nodes/planning.py::entity_source_binding` and its continuation protocol remain for the independent old entry only. Normal page/API development never routes through it and never consumes `developmentContinuation`.
- `Frontend/.../EntityInfoPanel` and legacy EntitySourceBinding cards remain usable, but explicitly label that their result does not affect Endpoint API design, development readiness, or Build context.
- Regression: `Backend/tests/test_development_continuation_stream.py` drives real Graph checkpoints and final AG-UI frames for page/API targets with multiple entities; `test_development_continuation.py` covers atomic startup failure and one-time consumption; `Frontend/tests/workflowPreview.test.ts` covers ready and partial-confirmation cards.

| Change type                                   | Expected validation                                                                              |
| --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Documentation/instruction/index only          | No backend health, Vite check, build, or compile required unless explicitly requested.           |
| Frontend TypeScript/UI/Electron behavior      | `pnpm build` from `Frontend`; if a dev server is running, check the active Vite URL.             |
| Windows Electron package with bundled backend | Run `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File scripts/win_pack.ps1` on Windows x64. |
| macOS Electron package with bundled backend   | Run `bash scripts/mac_pack_x64.sh` on Intel or `bash scripts/mac_pack_arm64.sh` on Apple Silicon. |
| Backend Python behavior                       | Focused `python3 -m py_compile` or narrower test for changed Python files, plus `/health`.       |
| Backend API/agent behavior                    | `/health`; add focused endpoint/manual checks when behavior changes.                             |
| Application-level database credential encryption | From `Backend`, run `.venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_database_credentials.py tests/test_entity_design.py tests/test_entity_detail_plan.py` plus focused `py_compile`; from `Frontend`, run `pnpm test:database-crypto`, focused ESLint, and `pnpm build`; then verify `/health.database_encryption` contains only public metadata. |
| User-skill catalog, enablement, runtime, and ZIP import | From `Backend`, run `python3 -m unittest tests.test_user_skill_settings tests.test_user_skills tests.test_user_skill_documents tests.test_user_skill_imports tests.test_user_skill_runtime tests.test_builtin_skills` plus focused `py_compile`; from `Frontend`, run `pnpm test:chat-skills`, `pnpm typecheck`, and `pnpm build`; then check `/health` and the active Vite URL when present. |

### Workflow storage and backend-owned continuation

- `Backend/app/protocols/workflow/presentation.py` owns the single public `state`, scalar `summary`, document/diff references and task presentation without embedded execution source material. `projection.py`, `stream_events.py` and direct-modification projections share it; standard AG-UI `RUN_FINISHED.result` remains the protocol envelope, not a second Workflow state.
- `Backend/app/workspace/content_artifacts.py` stores immutable JSON under `.devagentstudio/artifacts/sha256/<hash>.json`. Material fields of at least 4 KiB become `{"$artifact": {artifactId, byteLength, mediaType}}`; identical content shares files and resolved objects during one backend read. Missing/corrupt references fail closed. `persistence/artifact_serializer.py` and `checkpoints.py` apply this at the LangGraph serialization boundary; `workspace/task_documents.py` applies it to Pending/Formal/Build Run DAG JSON while digests and validation operate on expanded semantic data. No migration or old reader is provided.
- `Backend/app/routes/workflow_artifacts.py` owns read-only `/workflow-artifacts/run`, a complete independent AG-UI lifecycle accepting `forwardedProps.artifact={workspaceRoot,reference}` with a 16 MiB read limit. `Frontend/src/renderer/src/service/workflowArtifacts.ts` uses the official SDK. `WorkflowRunCard/useWorkflowDocuments.ts` reads current confirmation documents; `AiChatPanel/hooks/usePlanningDocumentView.ts` reads only the visible requirement/technical document and requirement-confirmation Markdown references, keeps up to eight workspace/hash reads locally, rejects stale responses and exposes loading/error/retry without writing bodies into canonical state or chat history. `projection.py` publishes merged RequirementSpec/ProductPlan Markdown at the current `requirement_document` joint gate. `CodeDiffDetailPanel` reads a patch on user request. These local display reads never become execution inputs. Regression: `pnpm test:planning-documents`, `tests.test_requirement_document_projection`, `tests.test_workflow_storage`.
- `Backend/app/services/recovery_snapshot.py` publishes scoped `extensions.recoverySnapshot` through Lifecycle Get. Session/thread/latest Run, current checkpoint, live execution and explicit End determine the sole current Workflow; output only supplies presentation. The shared Frontend RecoveryStore rejects stale reads before restoring messages or enabling actions.
- `Frontend/src/main/sessionWorkflowArtifacts.ts` externalizes edited formal documents and confirmation Markdown by content hash, then returns an inline compact Workflow object for `sessionFileWriter.ts` to save in the message. There is no separate display file lookup on opening a conversation. `sessionHistory.ts` still reads at most ten recent records from the tail with a 4 MiB soft page budget that reduces record count; a single record is read in full even when it exceeds that budget, without a size-based placeholder. Hash verification is reserved for actual artifact-body reads; missing artifact files do not block reading the message's lightweight display fields. Referenced artifacts remain shared across checkpoints and DAG files, so they cannot be deleted with individual chat messages; no garbage collection or historical-format migration is introduced.
